# 任务：把音乐流水线上游三步（A 稿、珠子、Center）改成“换一首作品只改参数就能跑”

## 背景
项目目录 D:\musicclaude\_thread_evo_20261019，流水线说明见其中 DAG.md（N02 A 稿、N03 珠子、N04 Center）。现在这几步的提示词和脚本把作品写死了，换一首新作品跑不起来：
- N02 A 稿：D:\musicclaude\_bead_op61_20261020\gen_A\gen.py，提示词文件按作品复制（如 `*.op61.txt`），正文写死“肖邦《幻想波兰舞曲》降A大调 Op.61”。
- N03 珠子：D:\musicclaude\_bead_op61_20261020\chain.py 及其 prompts\（pool/gen/rev1/rev2/final/piece/fix 和结构规范），写死“肖邦《幻想波兰舞曲》Op.61”、“PTNA／塚田冻结十三段”等。
- N04 Center：_thread_evo_20261019\center_from_A.py、center_dag.py（WORK 字典只有 op60/op61）、centersel.py 里被沿用的 SPEC/FIT 提示词（专属性测试的 10 首候选曲目写死、写死“肖邦”）。
- 分段已有：_thread_evo_20261019\works\<作品>\segmentation.json。

## 目标
1. 建一个作品登记：_thread_evo_20261019\works\<key>\work.json，至少包含 作曲家、中文曲名（带书名号的写法）、调性、作品号、分段来源的说明文字、专属性测试用的候选作品清单（10 首左右，同作曲家）。先填 op60、op61 两份（内容从现有提示词里原样取）。
2. 上述三步的脚本和提示词改成从 work.json 读这些参数，不再写死作品、作曲家、分段来源、候选清单。作品 key 用命令行参数或环境变量传入，沿用这些脚本现有的风格。
3. 写一份说明：_thread_evo_20261019\DAG\新作品上游运行说明.md（中文），写清换一首新作品时要准备什么（work.json、segmentation.json）、按什么顺序运行哪些命令。

## 硬性约束（必须遵守）
- **不改实验语义**：对 op61 和 op60，改造后渲染出来的提示词必须和现在逐字一致（作品名等原来就是这首的地方替换后结果不变）。验证方法：离线渲染（不调用任何模型），和现有真实发出的提示词比对——对照物在 _thread_evo_20261019\DAG\prompts\N02_*、N03*、N04* 下的 模板/实发 文件，以及 D:\musicclaude\_bead_op61_20261020\runs\ 和 _thread_evo_20261019\runs\ 里对应调用的 prompt.txt / input.txt。若有任何字不同，要说明原因；能做到一致就必须一致。
- **不调用任何模型**（GPT、Gemini、Argon 都不调），不提交任何网络请求；只做代码和离线渲染。
- **只准修改这些文件**：D:\musicclaude\_bead_op61_20261020\ 下的 gen_A\gen.py、chain.py、prompts\ 里的文件（可新增模板文件）；_thread_evo_20261019\ 下的 center_from_A.py、center_dag.py、centersel.py、works\*\work.json（新增）、DAG\新作品上游运行说明.md（新增）。如需新增一个小的公共读取模块，放在 _thread_evo_20261019\work_meta.py。
- **绝对不要改**：_thread_evo_20261019 下的 evo.py、mg_call.py、gpt3_call.py、covjudge.py、v6*.py、lang_open.py、dag_src3.py、dag_run.py、tgraph4.py、line8.py、line_dag.py、draft9.py、segcheck.py、DAG.md，以及 runs\、evaluations\ 下任何已有文件——另一条实验正在用它们跑，改了会破坏实验。也不要删除或覆盖任何已有的运行结果。
- centersel.py 如被其它脚本 import，改动后它对 op60/op61 的行为必须不变。
- 改前先备份要改的文件（同目录 *.bak_param20261101）。
- 本机 Windows PowerShell 5.1 会把无 BOM 的 UTF-8 中文文件读成乱码，读写文本一律用 Python（encoding="utf-8"）或 `-Encoding UTF8`。
- 不动缓存保温机制和 watchdog，不新增定时任务或轮询。

## 自测
- op61、op60：渲染 N02、N03 各提示词、N04 各提示词，和对照物逐字比对，报告结果。
- 假想新作品：新建一份临时的 work.json（例如 key=op52，肖邦《第四叙事曲》f小调 Op.52，配一份临时的 segmentation.json，可以随便分 3 段，放在 _codex\param_20261101\tmp\ 下，不要放进 works\），渲染全部提示词，确认里面没有残留“幻想波兰舞曲”“船歌”“PTNA”“塚田”“Op.61”“Op.60”等字样（候选清单里列出的作品名除外）。测试完删除临时文件或留在 tmp 下。
- 详细日志写到 _thread_evo_20261019\_codex\param_20261101\log.md。

## 回报
最后只回报：成功/失败、改了哪些文件、比对结果（逐字一致的有几个提示词、不一致的有哪些及原因）、还没参数化的地方、风险。遇到必须改变实验语义或无法做到逐字一致时，停下来回报，不要硬改。

# DAG：一首新作品 → 最终的珠子 + 线

地位：和 `THREAD_SPEC.md` 同等。所有迭代都是在改这个 DAG；流程、提示词、模型、节点有任何改动，先改这里，并在文末“修订记录”里记一笔。

模型路由：所有 GPT 调用默认走 gpt3（GPT-6 Thinking max / GPT-5.6 Thinking max），gpt3 一报错（用量上限、保护信号或连续失败）就由 `evo.call` 自动切回 MimicGate（GPT-6 / 5.6-sol High），标记文件 `runs/gpt3_down.txt`；Argon、Gemini 的节点不受影响。

每个节点链接到提示词快照：“模板”是脚本里的原样，“实发”是一次真实运行发出去的完整提示词（Op.61）。改了提示词就跑 `python dag_export.py` 重新导出到 `DAG/prompts/`。

图例：✅ 能直接套用到新作品　⚠️ 有已知问题，见“状态”一栏　🧪 试验中，还没定为生产节点　🗄 已被替代，留作记录

```mermaid
flowchart TD
  W[新作品：曲名 + 小节数] --> N01[N01 分段<br/>查公开曲式表]
  W --> N02[N02 A 稿 ×24<br/>4 模型 × 不联网/联网 × 3]
  N01 --> N03[N03 珠子<br/>pool→gen→rev1→rev2→final→piece→fix]
  N02 --> N03
  N03 --> N03h[N03h 珠子去编辑句]
  N03h --> N03i[N03i 珠子语言：开放评审→只改语言→逐颗核对听感内容]
  N02 --> N04a[N04a Center：读 24 份 A 稿]
  N04a --> N04b[N04b Center：写关系句]
  N04b --> N04c[N04c/d Center：测专属性、解释力，挑一句]
  N04c --> N05[N05 写稿：主线→成文 ×3，盲读挑一篇]
  N01 --> N05
  N05 --> N06[N06 转关系图]
  N06 --> N07[N07 抽词义]
  N06 --> N08[N08 通用改写：不标关系、句中引用、压缩]
  N07 --> N08
  N08 --> N09b[N09b 人话改写：整条用说话口气重说]
  N07 --> N09b
  N09b --> N09[N09 开放评审：不给分类，问通不通顺、是不是人话]
  N09 --> N09c[N09c 按评审改]
  N09c -->|下一轮评审| N09
  N09c --> N09w[N09w 逐字精读：脚本按原文替换字词]
  N09w --> N10[N10 测量：覆盖 / 单独读 / 清单 / 盲评]
  N10 --> N10c[N10c 提覆盖：带接不上的珠子重写弱段，只留变好的段，可多轮]
  N10c --> N10g[N10g 守覆盖：逐段不得低于上一版]
  N10g --> N11
  N03h --> N10
  N11[N11 补漏：被≥2份盲评点名的段重写]
  N11 -->|再测| N10
  N11 --> N12[N12 合写：线和珠子逐字排列，只显示小节号]
  N03i --> N12
  N12 --> OUT[最终：珠子 + 线]
```

| N03i 珠子语言 | 开放评审（三步，说明一段多种听法是有意的）→ Argon 只改语言（意象、比喻、感受、几种听法、小节都保留，不合并、不删、不加）→ 两位评委逐颗核对听感内容是否一致，任一位说不一致就退回原文 | `bead_lang.py review/fix/check` | GPT-6、GPT-5.6、Gemini 3.8（评审）；Argon（改）；GPT-6、GPT-5.6（核对） | [评审](DAG/prompts/N03i_珠子语言_评审/模板.txt) · [改写](DAG/prompts/N03i_珠子语言_改写/模板.txt) · [核对](DAG/prompts/N03i_珠子语言_核对/模板.txt) | ✅ 11-01：评审给原珠子 7–8 分；改 36/43 颗，核对退回 11/9 颗 |
| N04a Center 材料 | 24 份 A 稿各自的一句中心理解（聚类结果只作参考） | `center_from_A.py` | GPT-6 | [模板](DAG/prompts/N04a_Center_归纳A稿/模板.txt) · [实发](DAG/prompts/N04a_Center_归纳A稿/实发_op61.txt) | ✅ |
| N04b Center 写句 | 先数材料里的对象（几篇提到、叫法是否一致）和材料自己用的关系动词，看结尾各方状态；再写最小完整关系句：名词说得出是什么，谓语对全曲成立、不把一方定成障碍，动词取自材料，不用“牵制/张力”这类万能词；给 3 种写法；自检“单看能否知道关于什么、放到别的作品是否也成立” | `center_dag.py write`（CDV=2） | GPT-6 | [模板](DAG/prompts/N04b_Center_写句/模板.txt) · [实发](DAG/prompts/N04b_Center_写句/实发_op61.txt) · [旧版](DAG/prompts/N04b_Center_写句/旧版模板_center_v3.txt) | ✅ 不给范例、不给历史候选。两次运行对象一致，谓语在相近几种之间变动 |

P = ("附件是 24 份关于{WORK}的文章，由不同模型（gflash = Gemini Flash，luna = Luna，gpt56w = GPT-5.6，gpt6w = GPT-6）按两种提示词写成"

     "请做三件事：\n"
     "1. per_draft：逐份用一句话说出它对整首曲子的中心理解（它认为这首曲子从头到尾在处理一个人的什么事），标明文件名。\n"
     "2. clusters：把意思相同或相近的中心理解归为一类，写出每类的概括、支持它的文件名和份数。\n"
     "3. centers：只从最有说服力的类里，给出 2–4 个 Center——优先是多份稿子、多个模型都独立得出的理解；只有一两份提到的偏门读法不要，"
     "除非它确实特别贴合这首曲子。每个 Center 按这个定义写：说清楚共同的困扰——什么对这个人重要（matters），又是什么使它难以安顿（hard）；"
     "不预告发展的路线和最后的回答（不要写他最后怎样了）。用贴近这些稿子原本说法的人话，不要改写成新奇的角度。\n"
     "只输出一个 ```json 代码块：{\"per_draft\": [{\"file\": \"…\", \"core\": \"…\"}], \"clusters\": [{\"summary\": \"…\", \"files\": [\"…\"], \"n\": 0}], "
     "\"centers\": [{\"center\": \"一句话\", \"matters\": \"…\", \"hard\": \"…\", \"support\": 0, \"models\": [\"…\"], \"why_convincing\": \"…\"}]}")

if __name__ == "__main__":

    for w, d in SRC.items():

        assert len(files) == 24, (w, len(files))

        jobs.append((f"cA_{w}", (lambda w=w, msg=msg, files=files: evo.call(f"cA_{w}", msg, prompt_text=P, attach=files, meta={"stage": "center_from_A", "work": w}))))

"""DAG node N04 (10-31, user: "先把这个 center 先试试"; the old N04b fed per-piece history candidates and an example

  write : from the A-draft consensus clusters (N04a, reused: evaluations/centers/A_<w>.json) write the minimal complete
          relation sentence that NAMES ITS DOMAIN (what the matter is about) — keep the concrete objects the drafts name.

          piece among 10 (centersel.SPEC). ids cds_<judge>_<s>.
  fit   : explanatory power + room to develop per segment (centersel.FIT). ids cdf_<w>_<judge>.
  report: evaluations/center_dag/<w>.md, candidates = new + the current Center (OLD).
usage: python center_dag.py write | spec | fit | report"""
import json, random, sys

OUT = evo.ROOT / "evaluations" / "center_dag"; OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
WORK = {"op60": "肖邦《船歌》Op.60", "op61": "肖邦《幻想波兰舞曲》Op.61"}

WRITE = """任务：为{WORK}确定 Center。Center 是一条“线”所围绕的那件人的事：什么对这个人重要，又是什么使它难以安顿。它是读者听完全曲回头看时，把各段组织起来的那件事。

写成一句“最小完整关系句”：
- 主语：一个或几个对象，用名词短语直接说出它们是什么。
- 要点出领域：读者单看这句话，就要知道这件事是关于什么的、落在哪种关系或处境里。下面材料里说出的具体对象，尽量保留它们本来的名字，不要换成更空的上位词；不能只剩形式上的词（只说“做事”“想念”“内心”“向外”，却不说是关于什么的）。
- 谓语：说清这几个对象之间的一个关系，这个关系从曲子较早处就成立，一直到结尾都成立。不要为了戏剧性默认冲突；材料里是冲突就写明。
- 不写人称和心理动词（他、我、想、怕……）；不写后来才出现的变化、认识、解决或结局；不用比喻和画面；不写问句；不超过 30 个汉字。

依据：下面是 24 份独立文章对这首曲子中心理解的聚类结果（括号里是支持的份数）。贴着支持最多的那一类写，不要换成新奇角度。如果另一类也有很广的独立支持，而且确实是另一件事，可以另写一个；同一件事换个说法只算一个。
{CLUSTERS}

每个 Center 先给 3 种写法，再选一种，并用一句话说出它点出的领域是什么。
只输出一个 ```json 代码块：{{"centers": [{{"options": ["…", "…", "…"], "center": "选中的一句", "domain": "这句话点出的领域", "basis": "依据哪一类、几份"}}]}}"""


# v2 (10-31, user on "失去后的自尊与行动力始终受回忆和脆弱牵制": 失去啥了 / 为什么是受…牵制而不是平等 / 牵制就是最精确的吗):

WRITE2 = """任务：为{WORK}确定 Center。Center 是一条“线”所围绕的那件人的事：什么对这个人重要，又是什么使它难以安顿。它是读者听完全曲回头看时，把各段组织起来的那件事。

材料：24 篇独立文章，每篇对这首曲子中心理解的一句话概括（文章来自不同的人，彼此没有商量）：
{CORES}

请按下面的步骤做，前三步是给你自己理清材料用的，也要写出来：
1. 对象：列出材料里反复出现的“对象”——这个人在意、失去、守住、渴望或抗拒的东西。每个写出几篇提到，并说明材料对它的叫法是否一致（例如有的说出了具体是什么，有的只说“某种东西”）。
2. 关系动词：列出材料用来描述这些对象之间关系的动词或说法（原词），每个写出几篇用到。
3. 从头到尾：这几个对象之间，有没有一个从曲子较早处一直到结尾都成立的关系？特别看结尾：到结尾时，各方是什么状态（被战胜、被接纳、合在一起、仍在拉扯……）？只算多数材料都支持的。
4. 写 Center：一句“最小完整关系句”，不超过 30 个汉字。
   - 名词：说得出它究竟是什么。材料里叫法一致的，用材料的叫法；不一致的，用支持最多、多数材料都能接受的说法，并在 why 里写明几篇支持；不能留下没有对象的空词（单说“失去”“牵挂”“过去”“某种东西”而不说是什么的）。
   - 谓语：必须对全曲成立，包括结尾；各方的地位按材料来——不要把一方定成另一方的障碍或附属，除非多数材料从头到尾都这样写；动词从第 2 步的材料用词里选，或者贴近它们，并在 why 里说明它为什么比其他候选动词更准确。“牵制”“张力”“并存”“交织”这类放在哪首曲子上都说得通的词，除非你能说明它比材料用词更准确，否则不用。
   - 不写人称和心理动词（他、我、想、怕……）；不写后来才出现的变化、认识或结局；不用比喻和画面；不写问句。
5. 自检：一个没读过这些材料的人，单看这句话，能不能说出这件事是关于什么的？把它放到另一首肖邦作品上是否也成立？如果也成立，就改得更贴这首曲子。

如果材料里另有一种理解也有很广的独立支持，而且确实是另一件事，可以另写一个；同一件事换个说法只算一个。每个 Center 给 3 种写法，再选一种。
只输出一个 ```json 代码块：
{{"objects": [{{"object": "…", "n": 0, "naming": "叫法是否一致"}}], "verbs": [{{"verb": "…", "n": 0}}], "whole": "从头到尾成立的关系，以及结尾时各方的状态",

def cores(w):
    A = json.loads((evo.ROOT / "evaluations" / "centers" / f"A_{w}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    return "\n".join(f"{k}. {x['core']}" for k, x in enumerate(A["per_draft"], 1))


def clusters(w):
    A = json.loads((evo.ROOT / "evaluations" / "centers" / f"A_{w}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    return "\n".join(f"- （{c['n']} 份）{c['summary']}" for c in A["clusters"])

FILE D:\musicclaude\_thread_evo_20261019\centersel.py 
"""Center selection mechanism (user 10-21 approved; no beads):
  pool   : A-draft consensus Centers (A_<w>.json) + previous research winners (rs top 2) + dd61 for op61
  spec   : specificity — the Center sentence alone, shuffled with other candidates; judge spreads probability over 10 Chopin
           pieces; score = probability on the right piece. (gpt6 + gpt56)
  fit    : explanatory power + room to develop — per work, all candidates in one call: per segment the commonly described
           musical character (from knowledge/research, not beads), then 自然/勉强/不能 for each candidate; plus development
           (P0 accumulation kinds, not resolved at the start). (gpt6 + gpt56)
  report : combined table -> evaluations/centersel/<w>.md
usage: python centersel.py pool | spec | fit | report"""
import json, random, sys
from collections import defaultdict
from statistics import mean
import centers, evo, seg

OUT = evo.ROOT / "evaluations" / "centersel"
PIECES = ["船歌 Op.60", "幻想波兰舞曲 Op.61", "第一叙事曲 Op.23", "第四叙事曲 Op.52", "幻想曲 Op.49", "摇篮曲 Op.57",
          "夜曲 Op.27-2", "夜曲 Op.48-1", "英雄波兰舞曲 Op.53", "第三奏鸣曲 Op.58 第三乐章"]
RIGHT = {"op60": "船歌 Op.60", "op61": "幻想波兰舞曲 Op.61"}
SPEC = ("下面每一句话，都是有人为某一首肖邦作品写的“这首曲子围绕的那件人的事”。请判断每一句最可能是为哪首作品写的，"
        "把 100 分概率分给下面 10 首作品（可以集中，也可以分散；如果一句话放在哪首上都说得通，就分散）。只根据这句话本身和你对这些作品的了解来判断。\n"
        "作品：" + "、".join(PIECES) + "\n"
        "只输出一个 ```json 代码块：{\"items\": [{\"id\": \"…\", \"probs\": {\"作品名\": 0}, \"why\": \"一句话\"}]}\n\n")
FIT = ("下面是为{WORK}的「线」（Thread）提出的几个候选 Center。Center 是整首曲子围绕的那件人的事：什么对他重要、是什么使它难以安顿。"
       "这首曲子按这些段落划分：{SEG}\n"
       "请先依据你对这首曲子的了解和公认的分析（不要编），逐段写出这一段公认的音乐性格和情绪（一句话）。"
       "然后对每个候选，逐段判断：用这个 Center 加上前面段落的经历，能不能解释这一段的情绪——自然（不费力、贴合）、勉强（要绕弯或忽略这一段的主要性格）、不能。"
       "再给每个候选打 development（1–10）：它能否自然长出这类理解的积累——原先挤在一起的两个要求被分清；一种给过他东西的办法显出限度；合理选择的代价；"
       "一次满足照出仍未满足；知道更多反而更尖锐——并且开头不会把话说完。最后给 overall（1–10：作为这首曲子的 Center 有多大说服力）。\n"
       "只输出一个 ```json 代码块：{\"segments\": [{\"bars\": \"…\", \"character\": \"…\"}], \"candidates\": [{\"id\": \"…\", \"by_segment\": [\"自然\", \"勉强\", \"不能\"], "
       "\"development\": 0, \"overall\": 0, \"note\": \"一句话：最贴合与最不贴合的地方\"}]}\n\n候选：\n")


def pool(w):
    out = []
    A = json.loads((evo.ROOT / "evaluations" / "centers" / f"A_{w}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    for k, c in enumerate(A["centers"], 1):
        out.append({"id": f"A{k}", "center": c["center"], "support": c.get("support"), "models": c.get("models"), "src": "原稿共识"})
    rs = json.loads((evo.ROOT / "evaluations" / "centers" / f"rs_{w}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    for k in ({"op60": (1, 5), "op61": (2, 6)}[w]):
        out.append({"id": f"R{k}", "center": rs[k - 1]["center"], "support": None, "src": "上轮比赛"})
    if w == "op61":
        out.append({"id": "DD", "center": json.loads((evo.ROOT / "evaluations" / "centers" / "dd61.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))[0]["center"], "src": "行动与做梦"})
    return out

                msg = SPEC + "\n".join(f"C{i + 1}：{x['center']}" for i, x in enumerate(it))
                (OUT / f"spec_key_{jn}_{s}.json").write_text(json.dumps(key, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
                rid = f"csp_{jn}_{s}"
                jobs.append((rid, (lambda rid=rid, msg=msg, model=model: evo.call(rid, msg, prompt_text=SPEC, model=model, meta={"stage": "center_spec"}))))
        evo.run_parallel(jobs, 4)
    elif ph == "fit":
        jobs = []
        for w in ("op60", "op61"):
            p = json.loads((OUT / f"pool_{w}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            for jn, model in (("gpt6", evo.G6), ("gpt56", evo.G56)):
                pp = p[:]; random.Random(f"fit{w}{jn}").shuffle(pp)
                msg = FIT.replace("{WORK}", centers.WORKNAME[w]).replace("{SEG}", seg.clause(seg.load(w), "note")) + "\n".join(f"{c['id']}：{c['center']}" for c in pp)
                rid = f"cfit_{w}_{jn}"
                jobs.append((rid, (lambda rid=rid, msg=msg, model=model: evo.call(rid, msg, prompt_text=FIT, model=model, meta={"stage": "center_fit"}))))
        evo.run_parallel(jobs, 4)
    elif ph == "report":
        spec = defaultdict(list)
        for jn in ("gpt6", "gpt56"):
            for s in (1, 2):
                r = evo.output(f"csp_{jn}_{s}")
                if not r:
                    continue
                key = json.loads((OUT / f"spec_key_{jn}_{s}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                for it in evo.parse_json(r)["items"]:
                    cid = key.get(it["id"]); w = cid.split(":")[0] if cid else None
                    if cid:
                        tot = sum(float(v) for v in it["probs"].values()) or 1
                        spec[cid].append(100 * float(it["probs"].get(RIGHT[w], 0)) / tot)
        for w in ("op60", "op61"):
            p = json.loads((OUT / f"pool_{w}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")); fit = defaultdict(list)
            for jn in ("gpt6", "gpt56"):
                r = evo.output(f"cfit_{w}_{jn}")
                if r:
                    for c in evo.parse_json(r)["candidates"]:
                        bs = c.get("by_segment") or []
                        fit[c["id"]].append({"nat": bs.count("自然") / max(1, len(bs)), "no": bs.count("不能"), "dev": float(c.get("development", 0)),
                                             "ov": float(c.get("overall", 0)), "note": c.get("note", "")})
            md = [f"# {w}｜Center 选择", "", "| 候选 | 来源 | 原稿支持 | 专属性（猜中概率） | 解释力（自然段比例） | 不能解释的段 | 展开空间 | 说服力 | Center |", "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|"]
            rows = []
            for c in p:
                f = fit.get(c["id"], []); sp = spec.get(f"{w}:{c['id']}", [])
                row = (c["id"], c["src"], c.get("support") or "", mean(sp) if sp else float("nan"), mean([x["nat"] for x in f]) if f else float("nan"),
                       mean([x["no"] for x in f]) if f else float("nan"), mean([x["dev"] for x in f]) if f else float("nan"), mean([x["ov"] for x in f]) if f else float("nan"), c["center"])
                rows.append(row)
            for r in sorted(rows, key=lambda r: -(r[7] if r[7] == r[7] else 0)):
                md.append(f"| {r[0]} | {r[1]} | {r[2]} | {r[3]:.0f}% | {100 * r[4]:.0f}% | {r[5]:.1f} | {r[6]:.1f} | {r[7]:.1f} | {r[8]} |")
            (OUT / f"{w}.md").write_text("\n".join(md), encoding="utf-8"); print("\n".join(md)); print()


ROOT D:\musicclaude\_bead_op61_20261020

FILE D:\musicclaude\_bead_op61_20261020\gen_A\gen.py 
"""Generate missing Op.61 A originals via gpt3 (same A_int_v1 / A_web_v2 prompts as the A corpus)."""
import json, re, subprocess, sys, threading, time
from pathlib import Path
D = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
GPT3 = "D:/musicclaude/gpt2agent-safechain/bin/gpt3.cmd"
JOBS = [(f"gpt6w-int-{i}", "A_int_v1", "gpt-6-thinking") for i in (1, 2, 3)] + \
       [(f"gpt6w-web-{i}", "A_web_v2", "gpt-6-thinking") for i in (1, 2, 3)] + \
       [(f"gpt56w-int-{i}", "A_int_v1", "gpt-5-6-thinking") for i in (2, 3)] + \
       [(f"gpt56w-web-{i}", "A_web_v2", "gpt-5-6-thinking") for i in (2, 3)]
lock = threading.Lock(); last = [0.0]
def run(name, pv, model):
    out = D / "out"; out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    if (out / f"{name}.md").exists() or (out / f"{name}.submitted").exists():
        print(f"[{name}] skip (exists/submitted)", flush=True); return
    with lock:
        w = 7 - (time.time() - last[0])
        if w > 0: time.sleep(w)
        last[0] = time.time()
    (out / f"{name}.submitted").write_text(time.ctime())
    cp = subprocess.run([GPT3, "ask", "--file", str(D / f"{pv}.op61.txt"), "--model", model, "--json"], capture_output=True)
    (out / f"{name}.stdout.bin").write_bytes(cp.stdout); (out / f"{name}.stderr.bin").write_bytes(cp.stderr)
    m = re.search(rb'"job_directory":\s*"((?:[^"\]|\.)*)"', cp.stdout)
    job = Path(json.loads(b'"' + m.group(1) + b'"')) if m else None
    if cp.returncode == 0 and job and (job / "response.txt").exists() and b'"completed": true' in cp.stdout:
        resp = (job / "response.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        tv = json.loads((job / "terminal-verification.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        jr = json.loads((job / "result.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        fm = (tv.get("final_messages") or [{}])[0]
        meta = {"name": name, "prompt": pv, "model_req": model, "model": fm.get("model"), "effort": fm.get("effort"),
                "tools_used": jr.get("toolsUsed"), "temporary": jr.get("temporary"), "unpersonalized": jr.get("unpersonalized"),
                "elapsed_seconds": jr.get("elapsed_seconds"), "job_directory": str(job)}
        (out / f"{name}.meta.json").write_text(json.dumps(meta, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
        (out / f"{name}.md").write_text(resp, encoding="utf-8")
        print(f"[{name}] ok {meta['model']}/{meta['effort']} tools={meta['tools_used']} {len(resp)}ch", flush=True)
    else:
        print(f"[{name}] FAILED rc={cp.returncode} job={job}", flush=True)
ts = [threading.Thread(target=run, args=j) for j in JOBS]
[t.start() for t in ts]; [t.join() for t in ts]


FILE D:\musicclaude\_bead_op61_20261020\chain.py 
"""Op.61 copy of bead_chain/chain.py (only the pool stage is used here; Op.60 original untouched).
Op.60 A Pool -> Bead/STRUCT reusable chain (all model work via local gpt3 / GPT Web).

Stages (each cached per segment under runs/<tag>/<stage>/):
  pool   : gpt3 extracts every A passage applicable to the segment (exhaustive ledger)
  synth  : gpt3 turns pool -> STRUCT + reader bead (every pool item must be dispositioned)
  audit  : independent gpt3 audit; compares blind-labelled versions against its own experience list
  report : deterministic checks + metrics + deliverable assembly (no model, no human edits)

Usage:
  python chain.py pool   --tag v2 --segs S02,S07
  python chain.py synth  --tag v2 --segs S02,S07
  python chain.py audit  --tag v2 --segs S02,S07 --targets short,v1,v2
  python chain.py report --tag v2
  python chain.py all    --tag v2 --segs all --targets short,v1,v2
Prompt versions are chosen in chain_config.json.
"""
import argparse, hashlib, json, random, re, subprocess, sys, threading, time, zipfile
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
CFG = json.loads((ROOT / "chain_config.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
GPT3 = CFG["gpt3"]
INP = ROOT / "inputs"
SEGS = {f"S{i+1:02d}": (mm, lab) for i, (mm, lab) in
        enumerate(json.loads((INP / "structure.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["frozen_segments"])}
SHORT = {b["segment"]: b for b in json.loads((INP / "short_beads_ref.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))} if (INP / "short_beads_ref.json").exists() else {}
A = {p.stem: p.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig") for p in sorted((INP / "A").glob("*.md"))}
assert len(A) == 24 and not any(x in k.lower() for k in A for x in ("muse", "opus", "sonnet")), "need the 24 non-Muse, non-Claude A sources"


def mmr(seg):
    return SEGS[seg][0].replace("-", "–")


def norm(s):
    return re.sub(r"[\s\*`“”\"'‘’「」『』《》，。、；：！？,.;:!?（）()\-—–…·]", "", s or "")


NA = {k: norm(v) for k, v in A.items()}


def quote_ok(src, q):
    q = norm(q)
    return bool(q) and src in NA and q in NA[src]


def span(s):
    m = re.findall(r"\d+", s or "")
    return (int(m[0]), int(m[-1])) if m else None


def overlaps(seg, measures):
    a, b = span(SEGS[seg][0]); sp = span(measures)
    return sp is None or (sp[0] <= b and sp[1] >= a)


def in_seg(seg, measures):
    a, b = span(SEGS[seg][0]); sp = span(measures)
    return sp is not None and a <= sp[0] <= sp[1] <= b


# ---------------- gpt3 ----------------
_lock = threading.Lock(); _last = [0.0]


def gpt3(prompt_text, attach, outdir: Path, name):
    for attempt in range(1 + CFG.get("retries", 1)):
        r = _gpt3_once(prompt_text, attach, outdir, name)
        if r is not None:
            return r
        time.sleep(30)
    return None


def _gpt3_once(prompt_text, attach, outdir: Path, name):
    outdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    pf = outdir / f"{name}.prompt.txt"
    pf.write_text(prompt_text, encoding="utf-8")
    with _lock:
        w = CFG.get("min_start_gap_s", 7) - (time.time() - _last[0])
        if w > 0:
            time.sleep(w)
        _last[0] = time.time()
    cmd = [GPT3, "ask", "--file", str(pf), "--json"]
    for a in attach:
        cmd += ["--attach", str(a)]
    t0 = time.time()
    cp = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True)
    dt = time.time() - t0
    (outdir / f"{name}.stdout.bin").write_bytes(cp.stdout)
    (outdir / f"{name}.stderr.bin").write_bytes(cp.stderr)
    m = re.search(rb'"job_directory":\s*"((?:[^"\\]|\\.)*)"', cp.stdout)
    job = Path(json.loads(b'"' + m.group(1) + b'"')) if m else None
    ok = cp.returncode == 0 and job and (job / "response.txt").exists() and b'"completed": true' in cp.stdout
    if not ok:
        print(f"[{name}] FAILED rc={cp.returncode} {dt:.0f}s", flush=True)
        return None
    resp = (job / "response.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    jr = json.loads((job / "result.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    tv = json.loads((job / "terminal-verification.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    fm = (tv.get("final_messages") or [{}])[0]
    meta = {"job_directory": str(job), "model": fm.get("model"), "effort": fm.get("effort"),
            "status": fm.get("status"), "terminal_completion_verified": tv.get("terminal_completion_verified"),
            "elapsed_seconds": jr.get("elapsed_seconds"), "tools_used": jr.get("toolsUsed"),
            "temporary": jr.get("temporary"), "unpersonalized": jr.get("unpersonalized"),
            "prompt_sha256": hashlib.sha256(prompt_text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
            "response_sha256": hashlib.sha256(resp.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
            "attachments": [str(a) for a in attach]}
    (outdir / f"{name}.response.txt").write_text(resp, encoding="utf-8")
    (outdir / f"{name}.meta.json").write_text(json.dumps(meta, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"[{name}] ok {dt:.0f}s model={meta['model']}/{meta['effort']}", flush=True)
    return resp


def parse_json(resp):
    m = re.findall(r"```json\s*(.*?)```", resp, re.S)
    return json.loads(m[-1] if m else resp)


def load(tag, stage, seg):
    p = ROOT / "runs" / tag / stage / f"{seg}.parsed.json"
    return json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if p.exists() else None


def bundle_A():
    z = ROOT / "runs" / "sources.zip"
    if not z.exists():
        with zipfile.ZipFile(z, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as f:
            for k, v in A.items():
                f.writestr(f"A/{k}.md", v)
    return z


def bundle_ref():
    z = ROOT / "runs" / "sources_with_short.zip"
    if not z.exists():
        with zipfile.ZipFile(z, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as f:
            for k, v in A.items():
                f.writestr(f"A/{k}.md", v)
            f.write(INP / "structure.json", "structure.json")
            f.write(INP / "short_beads_ref.json", "short_beads_ref.json")
    return z


def fill(tpl, seg, **kw):
    s = tpl.replace("{SEG}", seg).replace("{MM}", mmr(seg)).replace("{LABEL}", SEGS[seg][1])
    for k, v in kw.items():
        s = s.replace("{" + k + "}", v)
    return s


def prompt(stage):
    return (ROOT / "prompts" / f"{stage}_{CFG['prompts'][stage]}.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")


# ---------------- stages ----------------
def st_pool(tag, seg):
    out = ROOT / "runs" / tag / "pool"
    if (out / f"{seg}.parsed.json").exists():
        return
    r = gpt3(fill(prompt("pool"), seg), [bundle_A()], out, seg)
    if r:
        d = parse_json(r)
        for it in d.get("items", []):
            it["quote_verified"] = quote_ok(it.get("src", ""), it.get("quote", ""))
        (out / f"{seg}.parsed.json").write_text(json.dumps(d, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")


def st_synth(tag, seg):
    out = ROOT / "runs" / tag / "synth"
    if (out / f"{seg}.parsed.json").exists():
        return
    pool = load(tag, "pool", seg)
    if not pool:
        print(f"[{seg}] no pool; skip synth"); return
    items = [{k: v for k, v in it.items() if k != "quote_verified"} for it in pool["items"]]
    sb = SHORT[seg]
    r = gpt3(fill(prompt("synth"), seg, POOL=json.dumps(items, ensure_ascii=False, indent=0),
                  SHORT_TITLE=sb["title"], SHORT_BODY=sb["body"], SHORT_LEN=str(len(sb["body"]))),
             [bundle_ref()], out, seg)
    if r:
        (out / f"{seg}.parsed.json").write_text(json.dumps(parse_json(r), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")


def st_display(tag, seg, src_tag):
    out = ROOT / "runs" / tag / "display"
    if (out / f"{seg}.parsed.json").exists():
        return
    d = load(src_tag, "synth", seg)
    if not d:
        print(f"[{seg}] no synth in {src_tag}"); return
    st = {"timeline": d.get("timeline", []),
          "facets": [{k: f.get(k) for k in ("id", "measures", "placement", "dimensions", "claim", "image", "validity", "salience", "relations") if k in f} for f in d.get("facets", [])],
          "readings": [{k: r.get(k) for k in ("id", "summary", "facet_ids", "relation", "depends_on_performance")} for r in d.get("readings", [])],
          "carryover": [{k: c.get(k) for k in ("from_range", "claim")} for c in d.get("carryover", [])]}
    start = span(SEGS[seg][0])[0]
    r = gpt3(fill(prompt("display"), seg, STRUCT=json.dumps(st, ensure_ascii=False, indent=0), START=str(start)), [], out, seg)
    if r:
        (out / f"{seg}.parsed.json").write_text(json.dumps(parse_json(r), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")


def display_checks(tag, src_tag, seg):
    d = load(tag, "display", seg); s = load(src_tag, "synth", seg)
    if not d or not s:
        return None
    fids = [f["id"] for f in s["facets"]]; fm = {f["id"]: span(f.get("measures")) for f in s["facets"]}
    seen = []
    bad_place = []
    prev_end = None; step_errs = []
    for st in d.get("steps", []):
        sp = span(st.get("measures"))
        if not in_seg(seg, st.get("measures", "")):
            step_errs.append(f"out:{st.get('measures')}")
        if sp and prev_end is not None and sp[0] < prev_end:
            step_errs.append(f"overlap:{st.get('measures')}")
        if sp:
            prev_end = sp[1]
        for kind in ("now", "also"):
            for it in st.get(kind, []):
                seen.append(it.get("facet"))
                fsp = fm.get(it.get("facet"))
                if sp and fsp and not (fsp[0] <= sp[1] and fsp[1] >= sp[0]):
                    bad_place.append(f"{it.get('facet')}@{st.get('measures')}")
    seen += [it.get("facet") for it in d.get("base", [])]
    lines = [it.get("text", "") for st in d.get("steps", []) for k in ("now", "also") for it in st.get(k, [])] + [it.get("text", "") for it in d.get("base", [])]
    rids = [r["id"] for r in s.get("readings", [])]
    rseen = [it.get("reading") for it in d.get("readings", [])]
    return {"facets": len(fids), "missing": sorted(set(fids) - set(seen)), "duplicated": sorted({x for x in seen if seen.count(x) > 1}),
            "unknown": sorted(set(seen) - set(fids)), "readings_missing": sorted(set(rids) - set(rseen)),
            "carryover": f"{len(d.get('carryover', []))}/{len(s.get('carryover', []))}",
            "steps": len(d.get("steps", [])), "step_errors": step_errs, "placement_mismatch": bad_place,
            "line_len_max": max((len(x) for x in lines), default=0), "lines_over_34": sum(len(x) > 34 for x in lines)}


def render_version(target, seg):
    """Returns (reader_text, struct_text_or_None) for audit, stripped of source attributions."""
    if target == "short":
        b = SHORT[seg]; return f"{b['title']}\n\n{b['body']}", None
    if target == "v1":
        d = json.loads((INP / "baselines" / "v1" / f"{seg}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        rb = d["reader_bead"]; s = d["struct"]
        st = {"facets": [{k: f.get(k) for k in ("id", "measures", "kind", "claim", "note")} for f in s.get("facets", [])],
              "alternative_readings": [{k: r.get(k) for k in ("id", "reading", "conflicts_with", "depends_on_performance")} for r in s.get("alternative_readings", [])],
              "internal_sequence": s.get("internal_sequence", [])}
        return f"{rb['title']}\n\n{rb['body']}", json.dumps(st, ensure_ascii=False, indent=0)
    d = load(target, "synth", seg)
    if not d:
        return None, None
    rb = d["reader_bead"]
    st = {"facets": [{k: f.get(k) for k in ("id", "measures", "source_range", "placement", "placement_note", "dimensions", "claim", "relations") if k in f} for f in d.get("facets", [])],
          "readings": [{k: r.get(k) for k in ("id", "summary", "anchor_sources", "facet_ids", "relation", "depends_on_performance")} for r in d.get("readings", [])],
          "timeline": d.get("timeline", [])}
    return f"{rb['title']}\n\n{rb['body']}", json.dumps(st, ensure_ascii=False, indent=0)


def st_audit(tag, seg, targets):
    out = ROOT / "runs" / tag / "audit"
    if (out / f"{seg}.parsed.json").exists():
        return
    labels = ["甲", "乙", "丙", "丁"][:len(targets)]
    order = list(targets)
    random.Random(f"{tag}-{seg}").shuffle(order)
    mapping = dict(zip(labels, order))
    blocks = []
    for lab in labels:
        rd, st = render_version(mapping[lab], seg)
        if rd is None:
            print(f"[{seg}] missing target {mapping[lab]}"); return
        blk = f"## 版本{lab}\n\n### 读者正文\n\n{rd}\n"
        blk += f"\n### 内部结构化资料\n\n```json\n{st}\n```\n" if st else "\n（此版本没有内部结构化资料）\n"
        blocks.append(blk)
    r = gpt3(fill(prompt("audit"), seg, LABELS="、".join(labels), VERSIONS="\n".join(blocks)), [bundle_A()], out, seg)
    if r:
        d = parse_json(r)
        d["_mapping"] = mapping
        for e in d.get("experiences", []):
            for s in e.get("sources", []):
                s["quote_verified"] = quote_ok(s.get("src", ""), s.get("quote", ""))
            e["in_segment"] = in_seg(seg, e.get("measures", ""))
        (out / f"{seg}.parsed.json").write_text(json.dumps(d, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")


# ---------------- metrics ----------------
def audit_metrics(tag, segs):
    rows = []
    for seg in segs:
        d = load(tag, "audit", seg)
        if not d:
            continue
        ex = [e for e in d.get("experiences", []) if overlaps(seg, e.get("measures", ""))]
        good = {e["id"] for e in ex if e.get("validity") in ("solid", "plausible")}
        solid = {e["id"] for e in ex if e.get("validity") == "solid"}
        for lab, tgt in d["_mapping"].items():
            v = d["versions"].get(lab, {})
            rc = set(v.get("reader_covers") or []); sc = set(v.get("struct_covers") or []) if v.get("struct_covers") is not None else None
            allc = rc | (sc or set())
            errs = v.get("errors", [])
            rows.append({"seg": seg, "target": tgt, "n_exp": len(good), "n_solid": len(solid),
                         "reader_cov": len(rc & good) / max(1, len(good)),
                         "all_cov": len(allc & good) / max(1, len(good)),
                         "solid_cov": len(allc & solid) / max(1, len(solid)),
                         "dims": len(v.get("dimensions_present") or []), "readings": v.get("distinct_readings"),
                         "readability": v.get("readability"),
                         "err": len(errs), "err_types": [e.get("type") for e in errs]})
    return rows


def summarize(rows):
    by = {}
    for r in rows:
        by.setdefault(r["target"], []).append(r)
    lines = []
    for t, rs in by.items():
        n = len(rs); avg = lambda k: sum((r[k] or 0) for r in rs) / n
        et = {}
        for r in rs:
            for x in r["err_types"]:
                et[x] = et.get(x, 0) + 1
        lines.append(f"{t:6s} n={n} 正文覆盖={avg('reader_cov'):.0%} 总覆盖={avg('all_cov'):.0%} solid覆盖={avg('solid_cov'):.0%} "
                     f"维度={avg('dims'):.1f} 读法={avg('readings'):.1f} 可读性={avg('readability'):.2f} 错误/段={avg('err'):.1f} {et}")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def rel_errors(d):
    fm = {f["id"]: span(f.get("measures")) for f in d.get("facets", [])}
    bad = []
    for f in d.get("facets", []):
        a = fm.get(f["id"])
        for r in f.get("relations", []) or []:
            b = fm.get(r.get("to"))
            if not a or not b:
                continue
            if r.get("type") == "after" and a[0] < b[0]:
                bad.append(f"{f['id']} after {r['to']}")
            if r.get("type") == "before" and a[0] > b[0]:
                bad.append(f"{f['id']} before {r['to']}")
    return bad


def reading_errors(d):
    sup = {f["id"]: {x.get("src") for x in f.get("support", [])} for f in d.get("facets", [])}
    bad = []
    for r in d.get("readings", []):
        anc = set(r.get("anchor_sources") or [])
        if not anc:
            continue
        for fid in r.get("facet_ids", []):
            if fid in sup and not (sup[fid] & anc):
                bad.append(f"{r['id']}:{fid}")
    return bad


def st_report(tag, segs):
    rows = audit_metrics(tag, segs)
    out = ROOT / "runs" / tag
    (out / "audit_metrics.json").write_text(json.dumps(rows, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
    s = summarize(rows)
    (out / "audit_summary.txt").write_text(s, encoding="utf-8")
    print(s)
    checks = {}
    for seg in segs:
        d = load(tag, "synth", seg); p = load(tag, "pool", seg)
        if not d:
            continue
        fids = {f["id"] for f in d.get("facets", [])}
        pids = {it["id"] for it in (p or {}).get("items", []) if it.get("applies") != "none"}
        disp = {x.get("pool_id") for x in d.get("ledger", [])}
        q = [(s.get("src"), s.get("quote")) for f in d.get("facets", []) for s in f.get("support", [])]
        checks[seg] = {
            "facets": len(fids), "readings": len(d.get("readings", [])),
            "pool_items": len(pids), "pool_undispositioned": sorted(pids - disp),
            "pool_sources_missing": sorted(set(A) - set((p or {}).get("sources_checked", {}))) if p else None,
            "pool_quote_ok": f"{sum(it['quote_verified'] for it in p['items'])}/{len(p['items'])}" if p else None,
            "facet_quote_ok": f"{sum(quote_ok(a, b) for a, b in q)}/{len(q)}",
            "facet_out_of_segment": [f["id"] for f in d.get("facets", []) if not in_seg(seg, f.get("measures", ""))],
            "relation_direction_errors": rel_errors(d),
            "reading_unanchored_facets": reading_errors(d),
            "reader_unknown_facets": sorted(set(d["reader_bead"].get("facet_ids", [])) - fids),
            "reader_chars": len(d["reader_bead"]["body"]), "short_chars": len(SHORT[seg]["body"])}
    if checks:
        (out / "checks.json").write_text(json.dumps(checks, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
        for k, v in checks.items():
            print(k, v)


def st_build(tag, segs, out_dir):
    """Assemble deliverables verbatim from model outputs; no model calls, no human edits."""
    out = Path(out_dir); (out / "struct").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True); (out / "evidence").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    reader, diffs, ev = [], [], []
    for seg in segs:
        d = load(tag, "synth", seg)
        if not d:
            reader.append(f"## {seg} ·（缺失）\n"); continue
        for f in d.get("facets", []):
            for x in f.get("support", []):
                x["quote_verified"] = quote_ok(x.get("src", ""), x.get("quote", ""))
        (out / "struct" / f"{seg}.json").write_text(json.dumps(d, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
        rb = d["reader_bead"]
        reader.append(f"## {seg} · 第{mmr(seg)}小节｜{rb['title']}\n\n{rb['body'].strip()}\n")
        diffs.append(f"## {seg}\n\n短版：{SHORT[seg]['title']}（{len(SHORT[seg]['body'])}字）→ 新：{rb['title']}（{len(rb['body'])}字）\n\n{d.get('diff_vs_short','').strip()}\n")
        for st in ("pool", "synth", "audit"):
            m = ROOT / "runs" / tag / st / f"{seg}.meta.json"
            if m.exists():
                e = json.loads(m.read_text(encoding="utf-8")); e.update(segment=seg, stage=st); ev.append(e)
                for suf in ("prompt.txt", "response.txt"):
                    src = ROOT / "runs" / tag / st / f"{seg}.{suf}"
                    (out / "evidence" / f"{seg}.{st}.{suf}").write_text(src.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), encoding="utf-8")
    head = ("# 肖邦《船歌》Op.60 · 十四颗珠子\n\n沿用 PTNA／朝山十四段。正文为 gpt3（GPT-6 Thinking / max）链路逐段生成的原样输出，"
            "未经人工改写；情景与人物动作是聆听比喻，不是作曲家生平或唯一演奏解释。\n\n")
    (out / "READER.md").write_text(head + "\n".join(reader), encoding="utf-8")
    (out / "DIFF_vs_short.md").write_text("# 与旧短版的差异（文字为模型自述，字数由脚本计算）\n\n" + "\n".join(diffs), encoding="utf-8")
    (out / "EVIDENCE.json").write_text(json.dumps(ev, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
    for f in ("audit_summary.txt", "audit_metrics.json", "checks.json"):
        if (ROOT / "runs" / tag / f).exists():
            (out / f).write_text((ROOT / "runs" / tag / f).read_text(encoding="utf-8"), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"built {out}")


def par_map(fn, segs, par):
    sem = threading.Semaphore(par)
    def w(s):
        with sem:
            try:
                fn(s)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[{s}] ERROR {type(e).__name__}: {e}", flush=True)
    ts = [threading.Thread(target=w, args=(s,)) for s in segs]
    [t.start() for t in ts]; [t.join() for t in ts]


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("stage", choices=["pool", "synth", "audit", "report", "build", "display", "all"])
    ap.add_argument("--tag", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--segs", default="all")
    ap.add_argument("--targets", default="short,v1")
    ap.add_argument("--out", default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--from-tag", default=None, help="display: STRUCT source tag")
    ap.add_argument("--par", type=int, default=CFG.get("parallel", 4))
    a = ap.parse_args()
    segs = list(SEGS) if a.segs == "all" else a.segs.split(",")
    tg = a.targets.split(",")
    if a.stage in ("pool", "all"):
        par_map(lambda s: st_pool(a.tag, s), segs, a.par)
    if a.stage in ("synth", "all"):
        par_map(lambda s: st_synth(a.tag, s), segs, a.par)
    if a.stage in ("audit", "all"):
        par_map(lambda s: st_audit(a.tag, s, tg), segs, a.par)
    if a.stage == "display":
        src = a.from_tag or a.tag
        par_map(lambda s: st_display(a.tag, s, src), segs, a.par)
        rep = {s: display_checks(a.tag, src, s) for s in segs}
        (ROOT / "runs" / a.tag / "display_checks.json").write_text(json.dumps(rep, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
        for k, v in rep.items():
            print(k, v)
        return
    if a.stage in ("report", "all", "audit"):
        st_report(a.tag, segs)
    if a.stage in ("build", "all"):
        st_build(a.tag, segs, a.out or str(ROOT / "runs" / a.tag / "deliverable"))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

  fit   : explanatory power + room to develop per segment (centersel.FIT). ids cdf_<w>_<judge>.

OUT = evo.ROOT / "evaluations" / "center_dag"; OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

WRITE = """任务：为{WORK}确定 Center。Center 是一条“线”所围绕的那件人的事：什么对这个人重要，又是什么使它难以安顿。它是读者听完全曲回头看时，把各段组织起来的那件事。

写成一句“最小完整关系句”：
- 主语：一个或几个对象，用名词短语直接说出它们是什么。
- 要点出领域：读者单看这句话，就要知道这件事是关于什么的、落在哪种关系或处境里。下面材料里说出的具体对象，尽量保留它们本来的名字，不要换成更空的上位词；不能只剩形式上的词（只说“做事”“想念”“内心”“向外”，却不说是关于什么的）。
- 谓语：说清这几个对象之间的一个关系，这个关系从曲子较早处就成立，一直到结尾都成立。不要为了戏剧性默认冲突；材料里是冲突就写明。
- 不写人称和心理动词（他、我、想、怕……）；不写后来才出现的变化、认识、解决或结局；不用比喻和画面；不写问句；不超过 30 个汉字。

依据：下面是 24 份独立文章对这首曲子中心理解的聚类结果（括号里是支持的份数）。贴着支持最多的那一类写，不要换成新奇角度。如果另一类也有很广的独立支持，而且确实是另一件事，可以另写一个；同一件事换个说法只算一个。
{CLUSTERS}

每个 Center 先给 3 种写法，再选一种，并用一句话说出它点出的领域是什么。

WRITE2 = """任务：为{WORK}确定 Center。Center 是一条“线”所围绕的那件人的事：什么对这个人重要，又是什么使它难以安顿。它是读者听完全曲回头看时，把各段组织起来的那件事。

材料：24 篇独立文章，每篇对这首曲子中心理解的一句话概括（文章来自不同的人，彼此没有商量）：
{CORES}

请按下面的步骤做，前三步是给你自己理清材料用的，也要写出来：
1. 对象：列出材料里反复出现的“对象”——这个人在意、失去、守住、渴望或抗拒的东西。每个写出几篇提到，并说明材料对它的叫法是否一致（例如有的说出了具体是什么，有的只说“某种东西”）。
2. 关系动词：列出材料用来描述这些对象之间关系的动词或说法（原词），每个写出几篇用到。
3. 从头到尾：这几个对象之间，有没有一个从曲子较早处一直到结尾都成立的关系？特别看结尾：到结尾时，各方是什么状态（被战胜、被接纳、合在一起、仍在拉扯……）？只算多数材料都支持的。
4. 写 Center：一句“最小完整关系句”，不超过 30 个汉字。
   - 名词：说得出它究竟是什么。材料里叫法一致的，用材料的叫法；不一致的，用支持最多、多数材料都能接受的说法，并在 why 里写明几篇支持；不能留下没有对象的空词（单说“失去”“牵挂”“过去”“某种东西”而不说是什么的）。
   - 谓语：必须对全曲成立，包括结尾；各方的地位按材料来——不要把一方定成另一方的障碍或附属，除非多数材料从头到尾都这样写；动词从第 2 步的材料用词里选，或者贴近它们，并在 why 里说明它为什么比其他候选动词更准确。“牵制”“张力”“并存”“交织”这类放在哪首曲子上都说得通的词，除非你能说明它比材料用词更准确，否则不用。
   - 不写人称和心理动词（他、我、想、怕……）；不写后来才出现的变化、认识或结局；不用比喻和画面；不写问句。
5. 自检：一个没读过这些材料的人，单看这句话，能不能说出这件事是关于什么的？把它放到另一首肖邦作品上是否也成立？如果也成立，就改得更贴这首曲子。

如果材料里另有一种理解也有很广的独立支持，而且确实是另一件事，可以另写一个；同一件事换个说法只算一个。每个 Center 给 3 种写法，再选一种。
只输出一个 ```json 代码块：
{{"objects": [{{"object": "…", "n": 0, "naming": "叫法是否一致"}}], "verbs": [{{"verb": "…", "n": 0}}], "whole": "从头到尾成立的关系，以及结尾时各方的状态",
  "centers": [{{"options": ["…", "…", "…"], "center": "选中的一句", "domain": "这句话点出的领域", "why": "名词和谓语为什么这样选（几篇支持、为什么这个动词最准确）", "selfcheck": "第 5 步的结果"}}]}}"""


def cores(w):
    A = json.loads((evo.ROOT / "evaluations" / "centers" / f"A_{w}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    return "\n".join(f"{k}. {x['core']}" for k, x in enumerate(A["per_draft"], 1))


def clusters(w):
    A = json.loads((evo.ROOT / "evaluations" / "centers" / f"A_{w}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    return "\n".join(f"- （{c['n']} 份）{c['summary']}" for c in A["clusters"])

"""Center selection mechanism (user 10-21 approved; no beads):
  pool   : A-draft consensus Centers (A_<w>.json) + previous research winners (rs top 2) + dd61 for op61
  spec   : specificity — the Center sentence alone, shuffled with other candidates; judge spreads probability over 10 Chopin
           pieces; score = probability on the right piece. (gpt6 + gpt56)
  fit    : explanatory power + room to develop — per work, all candidates in one call: per segment the commonly described
           musical character (from knowledge/research, not beads), then 自然/勉强/不能 for each candidate; plus development
           (P0 accumulation kinds, not resolved at the start). (gpt6 + gpt56)
  report : combined table -> evaluations/centersel/<w>.md
usage: python centersel.py pool | spec | fit | report"""
import json, random, sys
from collections import defaultdict
from statistics import mean
import centers, evo, seg

OUT = evo.ROOT / "evaluations" / "centersel"
PIECES = ["船歌 Op.60", "幻想波兰舞曲 Op.61", "第一叙事曲 Op.23", "第四叙事曲 Op.52", "幻想曲 Op.49", "摇篮曲 Op.57",
          "夜曲 Op.27-2", "夜曲 Op.48-1", "英雄波兰舞曲 Op.53", "第三奏鸣曲 Op.58 第三乐章"]

SPEC = ("下面每一句话，都是有人为某一首肖邦作品写的“这首曲子围绕的那件人的事”。请判断每一句最可能是为哪首作品写的，"
        "把 100 分概率分给下面 10 首作品（可以集中，也可以分散；如果一句话放在哪首上都说得通，就分散）。只根据这句话本身和你对这些作品的了解来判断。\n"
        "作品：" + "、".join(PIECES) + "\n"
        "只输出一个 ```json 代码块：{\"items\": [{\"id\": \"…\", \"probs\": {\"作品名\": 0}, \"why\": \"一句话\"}]}\n\n")
FIT = ("下面是为{WORK}的「线」（Thread）提出的几个候选 Center。Center 是整首曲子围绕的那件人的事：什么对他重要、是什么使它难以安顿。"
       "这首曲子按这些段落划分：{SEG}\n"
       "请先依据你对这首曲子的了解和公认的分析（不要编），逐段写出这一段公认的音乐性格和情绪（一句话）。"
       "然后对每个候选，逐段判断：用这个 Center 加上前面段落的经历，能不能解释这一段的情绪——自然（不费力、贴合）、勉强（要绕弯或忽略这一段的主要性格）、不能。"
       "再给每个候选打 development（1–10）：它能否自然长出这类理解的积累——原先挤在一起的两个要求被分清；一种给过他东西的办法显出限度；合理选择的代价；"
       "一次满足照出仍未满足；知道更多反而更尖锐——并且开头不会把话说完。最后给 overall（1–10：作为这首曲子的 Center 有多大说服力）。\n"
       "只输出一个 ```json 代码块：{\"segments\": [{\"bars\": \"…\", \"character\": \"…\"}], \"candidates\": [{\"id\": \"…\", \"by_segment\": [\"自然\", \"勉强\", \"不能\"], "
       "\"development\": 0, \"overall\": 0, \"note\": \"一句话：最贴合与最不贴合的地方\"}]}\n\n候选：\n")


def pool(w):
    out = []
    A = json.loads((evo.ROOT / "evaluations" / "centers" / f"A_{w}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    for k, c in enumerate(A["centers"], 1):
        out.append({"id": f"A{k}", "center": c["center"], "support": c.get("support"), "models": c.get("models"), "src": "原稿共识"})
    rs = json.loads((evo.ROOT / "evaluations" / "centers" / f"rs_{w}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    for k in ({"op60": (1, 5), "op61": (2, 6)}[w]):
        out.append({"id": f"R{k}", "center": rs[k - 1]["center"], "support": None, "src": "上轮比赛"})
    if w == "op61":
        out.append({"id": "DD", "center": json.loads((evo.ROOT / "evaluations" / "centers" / "dd61.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))[0]["center"], "src": "行动与做梦"})
    return out

FILE D:\musicclaude\_bead_op61_20261020\gen_A\gen.py 
"""Generate missing Op.61 A originals via gpt3 (same A_int_v1 / A_web_v2 prompts as the A corpus)."""
import json, re, subprocess, sys, threading, time
from pathlib import Path
D = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
GPT3 = "D:/musicclaude/gpt2agent-safechain/bin/gpt3.cmd"
JOBS = [(f"gpt6w-int-{i}", "A_int_v1", "gpt-6-thinking") for i in (1, 2, 3)] + \
       [(f"gpt6w-web-{i}", "A_web_v2", "gpt-6-thinking") for i in (1, 2, 3)] + \
       [(f"gpt56w-int-{i}", "A_int_v1", "gpt-5-6-thinking") for i in (2, 3)] + \
       [(f"gpt56w-web-{i}", "A_web_v2", "gpt-5-6-thinking") for i in (2, 3)]
lock = threading.Lock(); last = [0.0]
def run(name, pv, model):
    out = D / "out"; out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    if (out / f"{name}.md").exists() or (out / f"{name}.submitted").exists():
        print(f"[{name}] skip (exists/submitted)", flush=True); return
    with lock:
        w = 7 - (time.time() - last[0])
        if w > 0: time.sleep(w)
        last[0] = time.time()
    (out / f"{name}.submitted").write_text(time.ctime())
    cp = subprocess.run([GPT3, "ask", "--file", str(D / f"{pv}.op61.txt"), "--model", model, "--json"], capture_output=True)
    (out / f"{name}.stdout.bin").write_bytes(cp.stdout); (out / f"{name}.stderr.bin").write_bytes(cp.stderr)
    m = re.search(rb'"job_directory":\s*"((?:[^"\]|\.)*)"', cp.stdout)
    job = Path(json.loads(b'"' + m.group(1) + b'"')) if m else None
    if cp.returncode == 0 and job and (job / "response.txt").exists() and b'"completed": true' in cp.stdout:
        resp = (job / "response.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        tv = json.loads((job / "terminal-verification.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        jr = json.loads((job / "result.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        fm = (tv.get("final_messages") or [{}])[0]
        meta = {"name": name, "prompt": pv, "model_req": model, "model": fm.get("model"), "effort": fm.get("effort"),
                "tools_used": jr.get("toolsUsed"), "temporary": jr.get("temporary"), "unpersonalized": jr.get("unpersonalized"),
                "elapsed_seconds": jr.get("elapsed_seconds"), "job_directory": str(job)}
        (out / f"{name}.meta.json").write_text(json.dumps(meta, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
        (out / f"{name}.md").write_text(resp, encoding="utf-8")
        print(f"[{name}] ok {meta['model']}/{meta['effort']} tools={meta['tools_used']} {len(resp)}ch", flush=True)
    else:
        print(f"[{name}] FAILED rc={cp.returncode} job={job}", flush=True)
ts = [threading.Thread(target=run, args=j) for j in JOBS]
[t.start() for t in ts]; [t.join() for t in ts]


FILE D:\musicclaude\_bead_op61_20261020\chain.py 
"""Op.61 copy of bead_chain/chain.py (only the pool stage is used here; Op.60 original untouched).
Op.60 A Pool -> Bead/STRUCT reusable chain (all model work via local gpt3 / GPT Web).

Stages (each cached per segment under runs/<tag>/<stage>/):
  pool   : gpt3 extracts every A passage applicable to the segment (exhaustive ledger)
  synth  : gpt3 turns pool -> STRUCT + reader bead (every pool item must be dispositioned)
  audit  : independent gpt3 audit; compares blind-labelled versions against its own experience list
  report : deterministic checks + metrics + deliverable assembly (no model, no human edits)

Usage:
  python chain.py pool   --tag v2 --segs S02,S07
  python chain.py synth  --tag v2 --segs S02,S07
  python chain.py audit  --tag v2 --segs S02,S07 --targets short,v1,v2
  python chain.py report --tag v2
  python chain.py all    --tag v2 --segs all --targets short,v1,v2
Prompt versions are chosen in chain_config.json.
"""
import argparse, hashlib, json, random, re, subprocess, sys, threading, time, zipfile
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
CFG = json.loads((ROOT / "chain_config.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
GPT3 = CFG["gpt3"]
INP = ROOT / "inputs"
SEGS = {f"S{i+1:02d}": (mm, lab) for i, (mm, lab) in
        enumerate(json.loads((INP / "structure.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["frozen_segments"])}
SHORT = {b["segment"]: b for b in json.loads((INP / "short_beads_ref.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))} if (INP / "short_beads_ref.json").exists() else {}
A = {p.stem: p.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig") for p in sorted((INP / "A").glob("*.md"))}
assert len(A) == 24 and not any(x in k.lower() for k in A for x in ("muse", "opus", "sonnet")), "need the 24 non-Muse, non-Claude A sources"


def mmr(seg):
    return SEGS[seg][0].replace("-", "–")


def norm(s):
    return re.sub(r"[\s\*`“”\"'‘’「」『』《》，。、；：！？,.;:!?（）()\-—–…·]", "", s or "")


NA = {k: norm(v) for k, v in A.items()}


def quote_ok(src, q):
    q = norm(q)
    return bool(q) and src in NA and q in NA[src]


def span(s):
    m = re.findall(r"\d+", s or "")
    return (int(m[0]), int(m[-1])) if m else None


def overlaps(seg, measures):
    a, b = span(SEGS[seg][0]); sp = span(measures)
    return sp is None or (sp[0] <= b and sp[1] >= a)


def in_seg(seg, measures):
    a, b = span(SEGS[seg][0]); sp = span(measures)
    return sp is not None and a <= sp[0] <= sp[1] <= b


# ---------------- gpt3 ----------------
_lock = threading.Lock(); _last = [0.0]


def gpt3(prompt_text, attach, outdir: Path, name):
    for attempt in range(1 + CFG.get("retries", 1)):
        r = _gpt3_once(prompt_text, attach, outdir, name)
        if r is not None:
            return r
        time.sleep(30)
    return None


def _gpt3_once(prompt_text, attach, outdir: Path, name):
    outdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    pf = outdir / f"{name}.prompt.txt"
    pf.write_text(prompt_text, encoding="utf-8")
    with _lock:
        w = CFG.get("min_start_gap_s", 7) - (time.time() - _last[0])
        if w > 0:
            time.sleep(w)
        _last[0] = time.time()
    cmd = [GPT3, "ask", "--file", str(pf), "--json"]
    for a in attach:
        cmd += ["--attach", str(a)]
    t0 = time.time()
    cp = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True)
    dt = time.time() - t0
    (outdir / f"{name}.stdout.bin").write_bytes(cp.stdout)
    (outdir / f"{name}.stderr.bin").write_bytes(cp.stderr)
    m = re.search(rb'"job_directory":\s*"((?:[^"\\]|\\.)*)"', cp.stdout)
    job = Path(json.loads(b'"' + m.group(1) + b'"')) if m else None
    ok = cp.returncode == 0 and job and (job / "response.txt").exists() and b'"completed": true' in cp.stdout
    if not ok:
        print(f"[{name}] FAILED rc={cp.returncode} {dt:.0f}s", flush=True)
        return None
    resp = (job / "response.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    jr = json.loads((job / "result.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    tv = json.loads((job / "terminal-verification.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    fm = (tv.get("final_messages") or [{}])[0]
    meta = {"job_directory": str(job), "model": fm.get("model"), "effort": fm.get("effort"),
            "status": fm.get("status"), "terminal_completion_verified": tv.get("terminal_completion_verified"),
            "elapsed_seconds": jr.get("elapsed_seconds"), "tools_used": jr.get("toolsUsed"),
            "temporary": jr.get("temporary"), "unpersonalized": jr.get("unpersonalized"),
            "prompt_sha256": hashlib.sha256(prompt_text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
            "response_sha256": hashlib.sha256(resp.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
            "attachments": [str(a) for a in attach]}
    (outdir / f"{name}.response.txt").write_text(resp, encoding="utf-8")
    (outdir / f"{name}.meta.json").write_text(json.dumps(meta, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"[{name}] ok {dt:.0f}s model={meta['model']}/{meta['effort']}", flush=True)
    return resp


def parse_json(resp):
    m = re.findall(r"```json\s*(.*?)```", resp, re.S)
    return json.loads(m[-1] if m else resp)


def load(tag, stage, seg):
    p = ROOT / "runs" / tag / stage / f"{seg}.parsed.json"
    return json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if p.exists() else None


def bundle_A():
    z = ROOT / "runs" / "sources.zip"
    if not z.exists():
        with zipfile.ZipFile(z, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as f:
            for k, v in A.items():
                f.writestr(f"A/{k}.md", v)
    return z


def bundle_ref():
    z = ROOT / "runs" / "sources_with_short.zip"
    if not z.exists():
        with zipfile.ZipFile(z, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as f:
            for k, v in A.items():
                f.writestr(f"A/{k}.md", v)
            f.write(INP / "structure.json", "structure.json")
            f.write(INP / "short_beads_ref.json", "short_beads_ref.json")
    return z


def fill(tpl, seg, **kw):
    s = tpl.replace("{SEG}", seg).replace("{MM}", mmr(seg)).replace("{LABEL}", SEGS[seg][1])
    for k, v in kw.items():
        s = s.replace("{" + k + "}", v)
    return s


def prompt(stage):
    return (ROOT / "prompts" / f"{stage}_{CFG['prompts'][stage]}.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")


# ---------------- stages ----------------
def st_pool(tag, seg):
    out = ROOT / "runs" / tag / "pool"
    if (out / f"{seg}.parsed.json").exists():
        return
    r = gpt3(fill(prompt("pool"), seg), [bundle_A()], out, seg)
    if r:
        d = parse_json(r)
        for it in d.get("items", []):
            it["quote_verified"] = quote_ok(it.get("src", ""), it.get("quote", ""))
        (out / f"{seg}.parsed.json").write_text(json.dumps(d, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")


def st_synth(tag, seg):
    out = ROOT / "runs" / tag / "synth"
    if (out / f"{seg}.parsed.json").exists():
        return
    pool = load(tag, "pool", seg)
    if not pool:
        print(f"[{seg}] no pool; skip synth"); return
    items = [{k: v for k, v in it.items() if k != "quote_verified"} for it in pool["items"]]
    sb = SHORT[seg]
    r = gpt3(fill(prompt("synth"), seg, POOL=json.dumps(items, ensure_ascii=False, indent=0),
                  SHORT_TITLE=sb["title"], SHORT_BODY=sb["body"], SHORT_LEN=str(len(sb["body"]))),
             [bundle_ref()], out, seg)
    if r:
        (out / f"{seg}.parsed.json").write_text(json.dumps(parse_json(r), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")


def st_display(tag, seg, src_tag):
    out = ROOT / "runs" / tag / "display"
    if (out / f"{seg}.parsed.json").exists():
        return
    d = load(src_tag, "synth", seg)
    if not d:
        print(f"[{seg}] no synth in {src_tag}"); return
    st = {"timeline": d.get("timeline", []),
          "facets": [{k: f.get(k) for k in ("id", "measures", "placement", "dimensions", "claim", "image", "validity", "salience", "relations") if k in f} for f in d.get("facets", [])],
          "readings": [{k: r.get(k) for k in ("id", "summary", "facet_ids", "relation", "depends_on_performance")} for r in d.get("readings", [])],
          "carryover": [{k: c.get(k) for k in ("from_range", "claim")} for c in d.get("carryover", [])]}
    start = span(SEGS[seg][0])[0]
    r = gpt3(fill(prompt("display"), seg, STRUCT=json.dumps(st, ensure_ascii=False, indent=0), START=str(start)), [], out, seg)
    if r:
        (out / f"{seg}.parsed.json").write_text(json.dumps(parse_json(r), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")


def display_checks(tag, src_tag, seg):
    d = load(tag, "display", seg); s = load(src_tag, "synth", seg)
    if not d or not s:
        return None
    fids = [f["id"] for f in s["facets"]]; fm = {f["id"]: span(f.get("measures")) for f in s["facets"]}
    seen = []
    bad_place = []
    prev_end = None; step_errs = []
    for st in d.get("steps", []):
        sp = span(st.get("measures"))
        if not in_seg(seg, st.get("measures", "")):
            step_errs.append(f"out:{st.get('measures')}")
        if sp and prev_end is not None and sp[0] < prev_end:
            step_errs.append(f"overlap:{st.get('measures')}")
        if sp:
            prev_end = sp[1]
        for kind in ("now", "also"):
            for it in st.get(kind, []):
                seen.append(it.get("facet"))
                fsp = fm.get(it.get("facet"))
                if sp and fsp and not (fsp[0] <= sp[1] and fsp[1] >= sp[0]):
                    bad_place.append(f"{it.get('facet')}@{st.get('measures')}")
    seen += [it.get("facet") for it in d.get("base", [])]
    lines = [it.get("text", "") for st in d.get("steps", []) for k in ("now", "also") for it in st.get(k, [])] + [it.get("text", "") for it in d.get("base", [])]
    rids = [r["id"] for r in s.get("readings", [])]
    rseen = [it.get("reading") for it in d.get("readings", [])]
    return {"facets": len(fids), "missing": sorted(set(fids) - set(seen)), "duplicated": sorted({x for x in seen if seen.count(x) > 1}),
            "unknown": sorted(set(seen) - set(fids)), "readings_missing": sorted(set(rids) - set(rseen)),
            "carryover": f"{len(d.get('carryover', []))}/{len(s.get('carryover', []))}",
            "steps": len(d.get("steps", [])), "step_errors": step_errs, "placement_mismatch": bad_place,
            "line_len_max": max((len(x) for x in lines), default=0), "lines_over_34": sum(len(x) > 34 for x in lines)}


def render_version(target, seg):
    """Returns (reader_text, struct_text_or_None) for audit, stripped of source attributions."""
    if target == "short":
        b = SHORT[seg]; return f"{b['title']}\n\n{b['body']}", None
    if target == "v1":
        d = json.loads((INP / "baselines" / "v1" / f"{seg}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        rb = d["reader_bead"]; s = d["struct"]
        st = {"facets": [{k: f.get(k) for k in ("id", "measures", "kind", "claim", "note")} for f in s.get("facets", [])],
              "alternative_readings": [{k: r.get(k) for k in ("id", "reading", "conflicts_with", "depends_on_performance")} for r in s.get("alternative_readings", [])],
              "internal_sequence": s.get("internal_sequence", [])}
        return f"{rb['title']}\n\n{rb['body']}", json.dumps(st, ensure_ascii=False, indent=0)
    d = load(target, "synth", seg)
    if not d:
        return None, None
    rb = d["reader_bead"]
    st = {"facets": [{k: f.get(k) for k in ("id", "measures", "source_range", "placement", "placement_note", "dimensions", "claim", "relations") if k in f} for f in d.get("facets", [])],
          "readings": [{k: r.get(k) for k in ("id", "summary", "anchor_sources", "facet_ids", "relation", "depends_on_performance")} for r in d.get("readings", [])],
          "timeline": d.get("timeline", [])}
    return f"{rb['title']}\n\n{rb['body']}", json.dumps(st, ensure_ascii=False, indent=0)


def st_audit(tag, seg, targets):
    out = ROOT / "runs" / tag / "audit"
    if (out / f"{seg}.parsed.json").exists():
        return
    labels = ["甲", "乙", "丙", "丁"][:len(targets)]
    order = list(targets)
    random.Random(f"{tag}-{seg}").shuffle(order)
    mapping = dict(zip(labels, order))
    blocks = []
    for lab in labels:
        rd, st = render_version(mapping[lab], seg)
        if rd is None:
            print(f"[{seg}] missing target {mapping[lab]}"); return
        blk = f"## 版本{lab}\n\n### 读者正文\n\n{rd}\n"
        blk += f"\n### 内部结构化资料\n\n```json\n{st}\n```\n" if st else "\n（此版本没有内部结构化资料）\n"
        blocks.append(blk)
    r = gpt3(fill(prompt("audit"), seg, LABELS="、".join(labels), VERSIONS="\n".join(blocks)), [bundle_A()], out, seg)
    if r:
        d = parse_json(r)
        d["_mapping"] = mapping
        for e in d.get("experiences", []):
            for s in e.get("sources", []):
                s["quote_verified"] = quote_ok(s.get("src", ""), s.get("quote", ""))
            e["in_segment"] = in_seg(seg, e.get("measures", ""))
        (out / f"{seg}.parsed.json").write_text(json.dumps(d, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")


# ---------------- metrics ----------------
def audit_metrics(tag, segs):
    rows = []
    for seg in segs:
        d = load(tag, "audit", seg)
        if not d:
            continue
        ex = [e for e in d.get("experiences", []) if overlaps(seg, e.get("measures", ""))]
        good = {e["id"] for e in ex if e.get("validity") in ("solid", "plausible")}
        solid = {e["id"] for e in ex if e.get("validity") == "solid"}
        for lab, tgt in d["_mapping"].items():
            v = d["versions"].get(lab, {})
            rc = set(v.get("reader_covers") or []); sc = set(v.get("struct_covers") or []) if v.get("struct_covers") is not None else None
            allc = rc | (sc or set())
            errs = v.get("errors", [])
            rows.append({"seg": seg, "target": tgt, "n_exp": len(good), "n_solid": len(solid),
                         "reader_cov": len(rc & good) / max(1, len(good)),
                         "all_cov": len(allc & good) / max(1, len(good)),
                         "solid_cov": len(allc & solid) / max(1, len(solid)),
                         "dims": len(v.get("dimensions_present") or []), "readings": v.get("distinct_readings"),
                         "readability": v.get("readability"),
                         "err": len(errs), "err_types": [e.get("type") for e in errs]})
    return rows


def summarize(rows):
    by = {}
    for r in rows:
        by.setdefault(r["target"], []).append(r)
    lines = []
    for t, rs in by.items():
        n = len(rs); avg = lambda k: sum((r[k] or 0) for r in rs) / n
        et = {}
        for r in rs:
            for x in r["err_types"]:
                et[x] = et.get(x, 0) + 1
        lines.append(f"{t:6s} n={n} 正文覆盖={avg('reader_cov'):.0%} 总覆盖={avg('all_cov'):.0%} solid覆盖={avg('solid_cov'):.0%} "
                     f"维度={avg('dims'):.1f} 读法={avg('readings'):.1f} 可读性={avg('readability'):.2f} 错误/段={avg('err'):.1f} {et}")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def rel_errors(d):
    fm = {f["id"]: span(f.get("measures")) for f in d.get("facets", [])}
    bad = []
    for f in d.get("facets", []):
        a = fm.get(f["id"])
        for r in f.get("relations", []) or []:
            b = fm.get(r.get("to"))
            if not a or not b:
                continue
            if r.get("type") == "after" and a[0] < b[0]:
                bad.append(f"{f['id']} after {r['to']}")
            if r.get("type") == "before" and a[0] > b[0]:
                bad.append(f"{f['id']} before {r['to']}")
    return bad


def reading_errors(d):
    sup = {f["id"]: {x.get("src") for x in f.get("support", [])} for f in d.get("facets", [])}
    bad = []
    for r in d.get("readings", []):
        anc = set(r.get("anchor_sources") or [])
        if not anc:
            continue
        for fid in r.get("facet_ids", []):
            if fid in sup and not (sup[fid] & anc):
                bad.append(f"{r['id']}:{fid}")
    return bad


def st_report(tag, segs):
    rows = audit_metrics(tag, segs)
    out = ROOT / "runs" / tag
    (out / "audit_metrics.json").write_text(json.dumps(rows, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
    s = summarize(rows)
    (out / "audit_summary.txt").write_text(s, encoding="utf-8")
    print(s)
    checks = {}
    for seg in segs:
        d = load(tag, "synth", seg); p = load(tag, "pool", seg)
        if not d:
            continue
        fids = {f["id"] for f in d.get("facets", [])}
        pids = {it["id"] for it in (p or {}).get("items", []) if it.get("applies") != "none"}
        disp = {x.get("pool_id") for x in d.get("ledger", [])}
        q = [(s.get("src"), s.get("quote")) for f in d.get("facets", []) for s in f.get("support", [])]
        checks[seg] = {
            "facets": len(fids), "readings": len(d.get("readings", [])),
            "pool_items": len(pids), "pool_undispositioned": sorted(pids - disp),
            "pool_sources_missing": sorted(set(A) - set((p or {}).get("sources_checked", {}))) if p else None,
            "pool_quote_ok": f"{sum(it['quote_verified'] for it in p['items'])}/{len(p['items'])}" if p else None,
            "facet_quote_ok": f"{sum(quote_ok(a, b) for a, b in q)}/{len(q)}",
            "facet_out_of_segment": [f["id"] for f in d.get("facets", []) if not in_seg(seg, f.get("measures", ""))],
            "relation_direction_errors": rel_errors(d),
            "reading_unanchored_facets": reading_errors(d),
            "reader_unknown_facets": sorted(set(d["reader_bead"].get("facet_ids", [])) - fids),
            "reader_chars": len(d["reader_bead"]["body"]), "short_chars": len(SHORT[seg]["body"])}
    if checks:
        (out / "checks.json").write_text(json.dumps(checks, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
        for k, v in checks.items():
            print(k, v)


def st_build(tag, segs, out_dir):
    """Assemble deliverables verbatim from model outputs; no model calls, no human edits."""
    out = Path(out_dir); (out / "struct").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True); (out / "evidence").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    reader, diffs, ev = [], [], []
    for seg in segs:
        d = load(tag, "synth", seg)
        if not d:
            reader.append(f"## {seg} ·（缺失）\n"); continue
        for f in d.get("facets", []):
            for x in f.get("support", []):
                x["quote_verified"] = quote_ok(x.get("src", ""), x.get("quote", ""))
        (out / "struct" / f"{seg}.json").write_text(json.dumps(d, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
        rb = d["reader_bead"]
        reader.append(f"## {seg} · 第{mmr(seg)}小节｜{rb['title']}\n\n{rb['body'].strip()}\n")
        diffs.append(f"## {seg}\n\n短版：{SHORT[seg]['title']}（{len(SHORT[seg]['body'])}字）→ 新：{rb['title']}（{len(rb['body'])}字）\n\n{d.get('diff_vs_short','').strip()}\n")
        for st in ("pool", "synth", "audit"):
            m = ROOT / "runs" / tag / st / f"{seg}.meta.json"
            if m.exists():
                e = json.loads(m.read_text(encoding="utf-8")); e.update(segment=seg, stage=st); ev.append(e)
                for suf in ("prompt.txt", "response.txt"):
                    src = ROOT / "runs" / tag / st / f"{seg}.{suf}"
                    (out / "evidence" / f"{seg}.{st}.{suf}").write_text(src.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), encoding="utf-8")
    head = ("# 肖邦《船歌》Op.60 · 十四颗珠子\n\n沿用 PTNA／朝山十四段。正文为 gpt3（GPT-6 Thinking / max）链路逐段生成的原样输出，"
            "未经人工改写；情景与人物动作是聆听比喻，不是作曲家生平或唯一演奏解释。\n\n")
    (out / "READER.md").write_text(head + "\n".join(reader), encoding="utf-8")
    (out / "DIFF_vs_short.md").write_text("# 与旧短版的差异（文字为模型自述，字数由脚本计算）\n\n" + "\n".join(diffs), encoding="utf-8")
    (out / "EVIDENCE.json").write_text(json.dumps(ev, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
    for f in ("audit_summary.txt", "audit_metrics.json", "checks.json"):
        if (ROOT / "runs" / tag / f).exists():
            (out / f).write_text((ROOT / "runs" / tag / f).read_text(encoding="utf-8"), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"built {out}")


def par_map(fn, segs, par):
    sem = threading.Semaphore(par)
    def w(s):
        with sem:
            try:
                fn(s)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[{s}] ERROR {type(e).__name__}: {e}", flush=True)
    ts = [threading.Thread(target=w, args=(s,)) for s in segs]
    [t.start() for t in ts]; [t.join() for t in ts]


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("stage", choices=["pool", "synth", "audit", "report", "build", "display", "all"])
    ap.add_argument("--tag", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--segs", default="all")
    ap.add_argument("--targets", default="short,v1")
    ap.add_argument("--out", default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--from-tag", default=None, help="display: STRUCT source tag")
    ap.add_argument("--par", type=int, default=CFG.get("parallel", 4))
    a = ap.parse_args()
    segs = list(SEGS) if a.segs == "all" else a.segs.split(",")
    tg = a.targets.split(",")
    if a.stage in ("pool", "all"):
        par_map(lambda s: st_pool(a.tag, s), segs, a.par)
    if a.stage in ("synth", "all"):
        par_map(lambda s: st_synth(a.tag, s), segs, a.par)
    if a.stage in ("audit", "all"):
        par_map(lambda s: st_audit(a.tag, s, tg), segs, a.par)
    if a.stage == "display":
        src = a.from_tag or a.tag
        par_map(lambda s: st_display(a.tag, s, src), segs, a.par)
        rep = {s: display_checks(a.tag, src, s) for s in segs}
        (ROOT / "runs" / a.tag / "display_checks.json").write_text(json.dumps(rep, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
        for k, v in rep.items():
            print(k, v)
        return
    if a.stage in ("report", "all", "audit"):
        st_report(a.tag, segs)
    if a.stage in ("build", "all"):
        st_build(a.tag, segs, a.out or str(ROOT / "runs" / a.tag / "deliverable"))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE D:\musicclaude\_bead_op61_20261020\prompts\gen_v1.md 
你在为肖邦《幻想波兰舞曲》Op.61 的一个音乐段落挑选给听者的体验内容。本次只处理 **{SEG}（第 {MM} 小节，{LABEL}）**。

全曲按 PTNA／塚田冻结的十三段：{SEGLIST}。分段已定，不讨论分段本身。本段在常见演奏中大约持续 {SEC} 秒（粗略估计，仅用于考虑听者的注意力；不同演奏差异很大）。

## 材料

- 附件 `sources.zip` 的 `A/`：24 份历史聆听原稿，文件名即来源 ID。它们是研究素材，不保证全对：小节号可能有误、范围可能过宽。
- 文末的 Pool：此前从这 24 份原稿中为本段登记的原文条目。原话已逐字核对；`→` 后的位置是登记者的判断，可能有误，有疑问请回原稿核对上下文。Pool 只是检索索引，不是必须保留的清单。

## 目标

不是覆盖率越高越好。目标是：让听这一段的人，在有限的时间和注意力里，获得足够丰富、鲜活、独特、确实值得感受的体验。一段可能只需要一个很好的侧面，也可能需要好几个；没有规定数量，也没有上限。一种体验即使成立，也不一定值得额外占用一次听者的注意力。

## 任务：按边际价值排序的侧面阶梯

1. 第 1 个侧面：如果听者只能接收一种体验，最值得给的那一个。
2. 第 k 个侧面：在前 k−1 个已经给出之后，新增价值最大的那一个。新增价值指新的具体体验——新的身体感觉、感官质地、人物动作或关系、心理处境、本段内部真实发生的变化，或真正不同的理解角度——而不是同一种体验换一个比喻。
3. 一直排到剩下的材料都没有明显新增价值为止；然后再额外列出最多 3 个"停止点之后"的候选（如果还有材料），用来展示收益为什么衰减。
4. 给出你建议的停止点 `stop_after`（保留前几个），结合本段时长和听者注意力说明理由。

### 每个侧面怎么写

- `text`：给普通听者读的自然中文，一般 1–4 句。尽量保留原稿中最鲜活的具体画面、动作、触感和说法；允许小幅组织、把相容的原话接得更自然，但不要全面重写，不要洗成心理学概念或抽象结论。如果几个场景在你看来共同指向某个道理（例如"因为太在乎，所以动作变轻"），这个道理写进 `internal_note` 帮助理解即可，不要用它替代最动人的那个画面。
- 不要把不同原稿各自的人物、动因拼成同一个人的连续经历或因果链。来自不同原稿的话放进同一个侧面，前提是它们确实在说同一件事。
- `sources`：支持此侧面的原稿逐字原话（每条不超过 60 字，逐字照抄），注明来源 ID 和 Pool id（Pool 里没有的写 null）。
- `measures` 与 `placement`：这个体验在本段哪里成立。`stated`＝原稿明说，`inferred`＝按音乐事件推定，`segment_wide`＝整段性的。不能比证据更精确。原稿定位可疑、或实际属于邻段的，不要为了增益而选入。
- `relation`：与前面侧面的关系，可以有多项，每项写明针对哪个侧面：
  - `新体验`：与前面基本不重叠；
  - `重叠但新增`：与前面某个侧面大量重叠，但新增了独特的身体感觉、心理动因或关系角度（在 adds 里说清新增的是什么）；
  - `可同时成立`：与某侧面不同，但可以在同一时刻同时被感到——不是二选一；
  - `替代理解`：与某侧面是不同的理解角度，听者通常择一而听；
  - `段内先后`：音乐内部真实发生的先后变化（写清从哪里到哪里）；
  - `演奏条件`：只在某种演奏处理下成立（写清条件，不当作作品此处无条件的事实）。
- `adds`：一句话说清它相对前面侧面新增了什么（第 1 个写它本身提供了什么）。
- `also_expressed_by`：其他原稿对同一体验的不同鲜活说法（逐字原话＋来源 ID），它们并入这个侧面，不另立条目。只是换了个比喻的变体放在这里。
- `gain`：你判断的边际价值：`高` / `中` / `低` / `几乎无`。

### 另外

- `set_aside`：Pool 中你明确不采用的内容，按理由分组：`位置错误或属于邻段` / `原稿缺乏依据` / `只是重复已选体验` / `过于抽象或空泛` / `其他`。每组列 Pool id 和一句理由。已并入某个侧面的条目不必再列。
- `internal_note`（可选）：比较材料时发现的共同点、差别或隐含关系，只供内部参考，不上屏。没有就留空，不要硬造洞见。

### 写作提醒

戏剧性、肯定、单一故事的写法本身不是缺点；问题只在于把没有证据的关系拼起来，或用抽象概括替代具体体验。不要套用一个全曲通用的人物故事（例如反复的"倾诉、珍惜、害怕失去、等待回答"）；只写本段原稿真正支持、本段音乐真正有的东西。

## 输出

只输出一个 JSON 代码块，**直接写在回答正文里**，不要生成文件或下载链接，不要用代码工具写文件，不要联网搜索。

```json
{
  "segment": "{SEG}",
  "ladder": [
    {"k": 1, "id": "F1", "text": "", "measures": "", "placement": "stated|inferred|segment_wide",
     "relation": [{"type": "新体验", "to": []}], "adds": "", "performance_condition": null,
     "sources": [{"src": "SRC-xxx", "pool": "P01", "quote": ""}],
     "also_expressed_by": [{"src": "SRC-xxx", "quote": ""}],
     "gain": "高"}
  ],
  "stop_after": 0,
  "stop_reason": "",
  "set_aside": [{"reason": "", "pool": ["P03"], "note": ""}],
  "internal_note": ""
}
```

FILE D:\musicclaude\_bead_op61_20261020\prompts\pool_v2.md 
你是研究肖邦《幻想波兰舞曲》Op.61 的资料整理者。本次只处理一个音乐位置：**{SEG}（第 {MM} 小节，{LABEL}）**。全曲按 PTNA／塚田冻结十三段划分，不讨论分段本身。

附件 `sources.zip` 的 `A/` 中有 24 份历史聆听原稿（文件名即来源 ID，如 `SRC-gpt6w-web-1`）。它们是研究素材，不保证全对：小节号可能有误（有的稿按错误的总小节数编号）、范围可能过宽、引语可能不可靠。

## 任务：为本段建立完整的原稿账本（Pool）

逐份读完全部 24 份原稿，把**每一处说到本段音乐的内容**登记为一条 item。这一步只登记、不评判好坏、不跨稿合并：

1. **一体验一条**：同一份原稿里，每个可以单独成立的不同感受、画面、动因、身体感觉、先后变化、演奏差异各登记一条；同一原稿对同一体验的反复描写合成一条（选最清楚的一句作 quote）。不同原稿说同一件事各自登记。纯音乐技术描述（调性、和声名称、织体）只有在它直接承载某种听感时才登记。不要漏掉有具体内容的体验，也不要把一句话拆成几条。一般每份原稿对本段登记 0–6 条。
2. **范围判断**：原稿给出的范围（`src_range`）照实记录。若原稿范围比本段宽或跨段，判断这句话真正落在哪几个小节（`measures`），并在 `range_reason` 写依据（原稿明说的小节、音乐事件的位置、上下文）。原稿小节号明显按错误编号时，按它描述的音乐事件对应到真实位置，并说明。
3. **排除登记**：原稿范围与本段相交、但这句话实际属于相邻段落的，放进 `excluded`，写明属于哪里。完全不涉及本段的内容不用登记。
4. **原话**：每条附原稿原话 `quote`，必须逐字照抄、不超过 40 字、不改标点以外的任何字。
5. **复述**：`experience` 用一句平实中文（不超过 50 字）说清这条在说什么体验（不要加入原稿没有的人物、场景或因果）。
6. **每份原稿都要出现在 `sources_checked`**，写它为本段登记了几条；没有就写 0。

## 输出（只输出一个 JSON 代码块）

**必须把完整 JSON 直接写在回答正文里**，不要生成文件、不要给下载链接、不要用代码工具写文件。字段 `range_reason` 只在原稿范围与判断不同时填写，否则留空字符串。

```json
{
  "segment": "{SEG}",
  "items": [
    {"id": "P01", "src": "SRC-xxx", "src_range": "原稿标的范围", "measures": "判断后的精确小节", "range_reason": "", "quote": "逐字原话", "experience": "一两句复述", "type": "感受|画面|动因|身体|变化|演奏差异|音乐描述|其他"}
  ],
  "excluded": [
    {"src": "SRC-xxx", "quote": "逐字原话", "belongs_to": "实际属于的小节或段", "reason": ""}
  ],
  "sources_checked": {"SRC-xxx": 0}
}
```
