你是一位独立审稿人，正在评估给听者的肖邦《幻想波兰舞曲》Op.61 段落体验内容。本次只看 **{SEG}（第 {MM} 小节，{LABEL}）**。

全曲按 PTNA／塚田冻结的十三段：{SEGLIST}。分段已定，不讨论分段本身。本段在常见演奏中大约持续 {SEC} 秒（粗略估计）。

附件 `sources.zip` 的 `A/` 里有 24 份历史聆听原稿，文件名即来源 ID。它们不保证全对：小节号可能有误、范围可能过宽或跨段。

这次审稿分两轮。**本轮你还看不到任何待审内容**，请先独立建立自己的判断基准：

## 本轮任务：列出本段值得听者感受的不同体验

逐份阅读 24 份原稿，找出它们关于本段音乐的、值得一个普通听者去感受的不同体验。

- 一种体验一条。不同原稿说的是同一件事，合成一条并列出多个来源；只是换了比喻的说法也合成一条。但如果两种说法大量重叠、其中一种却多了独特的身体感觉、心理动因或关系角度，分成两条，并在 `distinct_from` 说清差别。
- `what`：用一两句具体的话写出这个体验，保留原稿里的具体画面、动作或触感，不要概括成抽象情绪词。
- `best_quote`：原稿里最鲜活的一句原话（逐字照抄，不超过 60 字）。
- `sources`：每个来源的逐字原话（每条不超过 60 字）。
- `measures` 与 `location`：这个体验落在哪几个小节；`本段明确`／`大致在本段`／`可疑或属于邻段`。原稿范围宽或跨段时，按原稿描述的音乐事件判断它是否真落在本段。属于邻段的也列出来并标明，因为待审内容可能误收它们。
- `kind`：`静态感受`／`段内先后变化`／`演奏条件下成立`／`另一种理解角度`。
- `importance`：`核心`（听者错过它就没真正听懂这一段）／`有价值`／`次要`。
- `vivid`：原稿表达本身是否特别鲜活、值得直接使用：`是`／`否`。

只输出一个 JSON 代码块，直接写在回答正文里，不要生成文件或下载链接，不要联网搜索。

```json
{
  "segment": "{SEG}",
  "experiences": [
    {"id": "E1", "what": "", "best_quote": "", "measures": "", "location": "本段明确",
     "kind": "静态感受", "importance": "核心", "vivid": "是", "distinct_from": "",
     "sources": [{"src": "SRC-xxx", "quote": ""}]}
  ],
  "notes": ""
}
```


FILE D:\musicclaude\_bead_op61_20261020\prompts\rev2_v1.md 
现在请审下面这组待审内容。它是另一个写作者为 {SEG} 提出的一串"侧面"，按写作者认为的边际价值从高到低排列：如果只给听者一个，就给第 1 个；给两个，就给前 2 个；依此类推。写作者对每个侧面标注了它与前面侧面的关系，这只是写作者的说法，需要你核实。

请以原稿证据和你上一轮列出的体验清单为基准独立判断，不要因为写作者给了某种排序或关系就默认它对。

评审提醒：戏剧性、肯定、单一故事的写法本身不是缺点，不因此扣分。要指出的是：把不同原稿没有证明的关系拼成一个人的经历或因果链、用抽象概括替代具体体验、音乐位置不对、或只是换了比喻却当作新内容。

## 对每个侧面（按 k 顺序）

- `covers`：听者读完这个侧面后，真正获得了你清单中的哪些体验（只算有效传达，不算仅仅提到来源；部分传达的写进 `partial`）。
- `new_vs_earlier`：相对前 k−1 个侧面，它真正新增了什么。如果没有新增，写"无"。
- `case`：从下列选一个最贴切的：`新体验`／`重叠但有新增`／`仅换比喻`／`可同时成立`／`段内先后`／`演奏条件`／`位置有问题`／`依据不足`。
- `relation_check`：写作者标注的关系是否正确；不正确时说明（例如把可同时成立的体验误当成二选一、把段内先后变化压成静态感受、把演奏条件写成无条件事实）。
- `vivid`：1–5，语言是否保有具体画面和身体感（5 最鲜活）。
- `abstract`：是否出现空洞总结、心理学概括或被统一过度的解释：`否`／`轻微`／`明显`（明显时引出原文）。
- `conjuncture`：是否把不同来源、不同人物或不同读法拼成了一条故事或因果：`否`／`有`（有时引出原文并说明哪几个来源被拼接）。
- `location_ok`：`是`／`否`（否时说明依据）。
- `worth_attention`：结合本段时长，这个侧面是否值得多占听者一次注意力：`值得`／`勉强`／`不值得`。
- `problems`：具体问题，每条引出待审原文并给依据。没有就空数组。

## 整体

- `stop_recommendation`：你建议保留到第几个，以及为什么在这里停（新增内容、重复、注意力成本）。如果中间某个应删除而后面的应保留，也写出来。
- `missing`：你清单中重要（核心或有价值）却没有被任何侧面有效传达、而且值得补入的体验，写出体验 id、内容和理由。只列真正值得补的。
- `template_check`：这组内容是否在套用某种通用人物故事或写作习惯，而不是本段音乐和原稿真正有的体验？有就引出原文。
- `best_lines`：待审内容里特别鲜活、可以直接使用的句子（引原文）。

只输出一个 JSON 代码块，直接写在回答正文里，不要生成文件或下载链接，不要联网搜索。

```json
{
  "segment": "{SEG}",
  "per_k": [
    {"k": 1, "covers": ["E1"], "partial": [], "new_vs_earlier": "", "case": "新体验", "relation_check": "",
     "vivid": 4, "abstract": "否", "conjuncture": "否", "location_ok": "是", "worth_attention": "值得", "problems": []}
  ],
  "stop_recommendation": {"keep": 0, "reason": "", "drop_inside": []},
  "missing": [{"id": "E3", "what": "", "why": ""}],
  "template_check": "",
  "best_lines": []
}
```

## 待审内容（{SEG}）

{LADDER}


FILE D:\musicclaude\_bead_op61_20261020\prompts\struct_spec_v1.md 
## 最终 Bead / STRUCT 的要求

这是经过选择的体验内容，不是研究摘要。数量由本段音乐、表达之间的重叠和新增内容的边际价值决定；不规定条数，也不规定上限。

- `beads`：最终保留的每一种体验。
  - `text`：给普通听者读的自然中文，保留原稿中最鲜活的具体画面、动作、触感和说法；允许小幅组织和拼接相容内容，但不全面重写、不洗成抽象解释。
  - `role`：`主体验`／`另一侧面`（与其他体验可同时成立）／`另一种理解`（与某体验是不同听法）／`段内变化`（音乐内部的先后变化）／`演奏条件`（只在某种演奏处理下成立）。
  - `measures`、`placement`（`stated`／`inferred`／`segment_wide`，不能比证据更精确）。
  - `performance_condition`：演奏条件，没有就写 null。
  - `sources`：逐字原话（每条不超过 60 字）＋来源 ID。
  - `also_expressed_by`：并入此体验的其他鲜活原话（逐字＋来源 ID），供以后呈现时替换使用；没有可为空。
  - `vivid_lines`：可以直接上屏的原稿原句（逐字），没有可为空。
- `relations`：只写真实存在的关系：`可同时成立`／`另一种理解`／`段内先后`／`重叠但新增`，并写一句说明。没有就留空，不必为了格式整齐每类都出现。
- `timeline`：只有本段内部确实有先后变化时才写（每步：小节、音乐事件、相关 bead）；否则为空数组。
- `reader`：给听者的连续自然中文正文，可以只选 beads 中适合当前呈现的内容。不同来源的不同理解要让读者看得出是不同的听法，不要串成同一个人的经历或因果链。不要套用全曲通用的人物故事。
- `reader_uses`：正文用到的 bead id。
- `title`：本段一个短标题（不超过 16 字），来自本段真正的体验。
- `stop_reason`：为什么停在这些体验，不再增加。
- `not_kept`：值得记录的、未保留的重要候选及原因（简短；只列有判断价值的几条）。
- `internal_note`：内部理解（不上屏），没有就留空。

戏剧性、肯定、单一故事的写法本身不是缺点；问题只在于把没有证据的关系拼起来，或用抽象概括替代具体体验。

只输出一个 JSON 代码块，**直接写在回答正文里**，不要生成文件或下载链接，不要联网搜索。

```json
{
  "segment": "{SEG}",
  "title": "",
  "beads": [
    {"id": "B1", "role": "主体验", "text": "", "measures": "", "placement": "stated",
     "performance_condition": null,
     "sources": [{"src": "SRC-xxx", "quote": ""}],
     "also_expressed_by": [{"src": "SRC-xxx", "quote": ""}],
     "vivid_lines": [{"src": "SRC-xxx", "quote": ""}]}
  ],
  "relations": [{"a": "B1", "b": "B2", "type": "可同时成立", "note": ""}],
  "timeline": [],
  "reader": "",
  "reader_uses": ["B1"],
  "stop_reason": "",
  "not_kept": [{"what": "", "why": ""}],
  "review_responses": [{"issue": "", "decision": "采纳|部分采纳|不采纳", "why": ""}],
  "internal_note": ""
}
```
（`review_responses` 只在有审稿意见时填写，否则为空数组。）

FILE D:\musicclaude\_thread_evo_20261019\seg.py 
"""Upstream segmentation stage -> machine-readable works/<key>/segmentation.json.

Two producers, one schema:
  import-op60          convert the existing published-structure upstream product (bead_chain structure.json, PTNA/C2)
  research <key>       GPT-6 Max (gpt3) extracts published structure tables and auto-selects one; script validates
Consumers never hand-type bar numbers: the Thread prompt's segment clause is rendered from this file (render_clause).

Schema: {work, short_name, total_measures, boundaries:[s1,...,sN,total+1] (right-open, last is a boundary, not a bar),
         segments:[{id,start,end,label}], source:{...}, method, alternatives:[...]}
"""
import json, sys
from pathlib import Path
import evo

WORKS = evo.ROOT / "works"
CFG = {
    "op60": {"work": "Frédéric Chopin, Barcarolle in F-sharp major, Op. 60", "ident": "单乐章，通行版本共116小节"},
    "op61": {"work": "Frédéric Chopin, Polonaise-Fantaisie in A-flat major, Op. 61", "ident": "单乐章，通行版本共288小节"},
    "op52": {"work": "Frédéric Chopin, Ballade No. 4 in F minor, Op. 52", "ident": "单乐章，通行版本共239小节"},
}


def validate(segs, total):
    errs = []
    if not segs:
        return ["no segments"]
    if segs[0]["start"] != 1:
        errs.append(f"first segment starts at {segs[0]['start']}")
    for a, b in zip(segs, segs[1:]):
        if b["start"] != a["end"] + 1:
            errs.append(f"gap/overlap between {a['start']}-{a['end']} and {b['start']}-{b['end']}")
    for s in segs:
        if not (isinstance(s["start"], int) and isinstance(s["end"], int)) or s["start"] > s["end"]:
            errs.append(f"bad segment {s}")
    if total and segs[-1]["end"] != total:
        errs.append(f"last segment ends at {segs[-1]['end']} != total {total}")
    return errs


def build(work, short, total, segs, source, method, alternatives=()):
    segs = [{"id": f"S{i+1:02d}", "start": s["start"], "end": s["end"], "label": s.get("label", "")} for i, s in enumerate(segs)]
    errs = validate(segs, total)
    assert not errs, errs
    return {"work": work, "short_name": short, "total_measures": total,
            "boundaries": [s["start"] for s in segs] + [total + 1], "segments": segs,
            "source": source, "method": method, "alternatives": list(alternatives)}


def save(key, d):
    p = WORKS / key; p.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (p / "segmentation.json").write_text(json.dumps(d, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"{key}: {len(d['segments'])} segments, boundaries {d['boundaries']}")


def load(key):
    return json.loads((WORKS / key / "segmentation.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def import_op60():
    s = json.loads((evo.ROOT / "inputs" / "op60_structure_published.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    segs = []
    for rng, lab in s["frozen_segments"]:
        a, b = map(int, rng.split("-")); segs.append({"start": a, "end": b, "label": lab})
    save("op60", build(CFG["op60"]["work"], "肖邦船歌", 116, segs, s["evidence"], "published_structure_select (bead_chain upstream, C2)"))


def research(key):
    c = CFG[key]
    tpl = evo.rd(evo.ROOT / "prompts" / "seg_published_v1.txt")
    msg = tpl.replace("{WORK}", c["work"]).replace("{IDENT}", c["ident"])
    rid = f"seg_{key}_v1"
    resp = evo.call(rid, msg, prompt_text=tpl, meta={"stage": "segmentation", "work": key})
    if not resp:
        sys.exit(f"{rid}: no response")
    j = evo.parse_json(resp)
    (evo.RUNS / rid / "parsed.json").write_text(json.dumps(j, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
    if j.get("insufficient_evidence"):
        sys.exit(f"{key}: insufficient evidence")
    sel = j["selected"]; total = int(j["total_measures"])
    segs = [{"start": int(x["start"]), "end": int(x["end"]), "label": x.get("label", "")} for x in sel["segments"]]
    errs = validate(segs, total)
    # The selected table must be one source's own level verbatim (no mixing).
    # Allowed: one level verbatim, or several of the same source's levels concatenated in order
    # (e.g. a source that lists sub-sections separately under each of its large parts).
    src = next((s for s in j["sources"] if s["id"] == sel["source_id"]), None)
    want = [(x["start"], x["end"]) for x in segs]
    lv = [[(int(x["start"]), int(x["end"])) for x in l["segments"]] for l in (src or {}).get("levels", [])
          if all(x.get("start") is not None and x.get("end") is not None for x in l["segments"])]
    if want not in lv and want != [p for l in lv for p in l]:
        errs.append("selected segments are not verbatim from one source")
    alts = []
    for s in j["sources"]:
        for l in s.get("levels", []):
            try:
                ss = [{"start": int(x["start"]), "end": int(x["end"]), "label": x.get("label", "")} for x in l["segments"]]
            except Exception:
                continue
            if not validate(ss, total):
                alts.append({"source_id": s["id"], "who": s.get("author_or_institution"), "level": l["level"],
                             "boundaries": [x["start"] for x in ss] + [total + 1]})
    (evo.RUNS / rid / "validation.json").write_text(json.dumps({"errors": errs, "valid_alternatives": alts}, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
    if errs:
        sys.exit(f"{key}: validation failed: {errs}")
    source = {k: src.get(k) for k in ("id", "author_or_institution", "title", "url", "locator", "accessed_original")}
    source.update({"level": sel["level"], "reason": sel.get("reason"), "numbering_note": j.get("numbering_note"),
                   "disagreements": j.get("disagreements"), "run": rid})
    save(key if key != "op60" else "op60_auto", build(j.get("work") or c["work"], j.get("short_name") or c["work"], total, segs, source,
                    "published_structure_select (gpt3 GPT-6 Max web research + script validation)", alts))


# ---------- rendering the segment clause for Thread prompts ----------

def clause(d, style):
    if style == "orig":   # exactly the user's original P0 wording style: boundary list incl. right edge
        return "按照小节数" + ", ".join(map(str, d["boundaries"])) + " 来分段"
    if style == "note":   # original list + one clarifying clause about the right edge
        return ("按照小节数" + ", ".join(map(str, d["boundaries"])) +
                f" 来分段（最后一个数{d['boundaries'][-1]}只是全曲结束的边界，不是一个小节；全曲共{d['total_measures']}小节）")
    if style == "ranges":  # unambiguous closed ranges; the final boundary is never presented as a bar
        rs = "、".join(f"第{s['start']}–{s['end']}小节" for s in d["segments"])
        return f"按照以下{len(d['segments'])}个小节区间分段（全曲共{d['total_measures']}小节）：{rs}"
    raise ValueError(style)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if sys.argv[1] == "import-op60":
        import_op60()
    elif sys.argv[1] == "research":
        research(sys.argv[2])
    elif sys.argv[1] == "check-beads":   # bead STRUCT must sit exactly on the upstream segmentation
        d = load(sys.argv[2])
        st = json.loads((evo.PKG / "03_Bead机器可读" / "14段_STRUCT轻量版.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["segments"]
        got = [(x["segment"], x["mm_start"], x["mm_end"]) for x in st]
        want = [(s["id"], s["start"], s["end"]) for s in d["segments"]]
        assert got == want, (got, want)
        print(f"beads aligned: {len(got)} segments, {sum(len(x['beads']) for x in st)} beads on {d['boundaries']}")
    elif sys.argv[1] == "show":
        d = load(sys.argv[2]); print(clause(d, "orig")); print(clause(d, "ranges"))


FILE D:\musicclaude\_thread_evo_20261019\centers.py 
"""New mechanism: Center grown from the beads, then a Thread written along that Center.
Step 1: python centers.py find <tag> <n_calls>      -> runs/cf_<tag>_<i>/, evaluations/centers/<tag>.json
Step 2: python centers.py write <tag> [<n_per_center>] -> runs/cw_<tag>_<k>_r<j>/ (P0 + center note + D3 material)
"""
import json, sys
import align, evo, gen

OUT = evo.ROOT / "evaluations" / "centers"


WORKNAME = {"op60": "肖邦《船歌》Op.60", "op61": "肖邦《幻想波兰舞曲》Op.61"}


def find(tag, n, work="op60", ver="v1"):
    btxt, beads = align.beads_text(work)
    tpl = evo.rd(evo.ROOT / "prompts" / f"center_from_beads_{ver}.md")
    msg = tpl.replace("{WORK}", WORKNAME[work]).replace("{BEADS}", btxt)
    evo.run_parallel([(f"cf_{tag}_{i}", (lambda i=i: evo.call(f"cf_{tag}_{i}", msg, prompt_text=tpl, meta={"stage": "center_find"})))
                      for i in range(1, n + 1)], n)
    cands = []
    for i in range(1, n + 1):
        r = evo.output(f"cf_{tag}_{i}")
        if not r:
            continue
        for c in evo.parse_json(r).get("candidates", []):
            ok = [e for e in c.get("evidence", []) if e.get("bead") in beads and align.norm(e.get("quote")) in align.norm(beads[e["bead"]])]
            c["evidence_verified"] = f"{len(ok)}/{len(c.get('evidence', []))}"
            c["segments_covered"] = len({e["bead"].split("/")[0] for e in ok})
            c["from"] = f"cf_{tag}_{i}"
            cands.append(c)
    OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (OUT / f"{tag}.json").write_text(json.dumps(cands, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
    for k, c in enumerate(cands, 1):
        print(k, c["evidence_verified"], "segs", c["segments_covered"], "|", c["center"])


def block(c):
    ev = "\n".join(f"- {e['bead']}：{e['quote']}" for e in c.get("evidence", []))
    hi = "\n".join(f"- {h['segment']}：{h['change']}" for h in c.get("history", []))
    return ("这一次，Thread 所围绕的那件事（Center）已经从这首曲子各段的体验记录里找出来了：\n\n"
            f"**{c['center']}**\n\n它在体验记录中的依据：\n{ev}\n\n它在各段经历中可能的变化线索（只是线索，不必逐条照写，也可以发现它不成立的地方）：\n{hi}\n\n"
            "请沿着这件事写 Thread：各段的转折尽量落在体验记录写到的真实体验上，让前面的经历成为后面感受的理由；"
            "不要另外编造事件、对白或物件来推动情绪，也不要按顺序把体验记录换个说法重讲。")


def write(tag, n_per):
    cands = json.loads((OUT / f"{tag}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    base = gen.render("P0", "op60", "orig")
    mat = gen.material("D3", "op60")
    jobs = []
    for k, c in enumerate(cands, 1):
        for j in range(1, n_per + 1):
            rid = f"cw_{tag}_{k}_r{j}"
            msg = base + "\n\n" + block(c) + mat
            jobs.append((rid, (lambda rid=rid, msg=msg, k=k: evo.call(rid, msg, prompt_text=block(cands[k - 1]),
                                                                     meta={"stage": "center_write", "center": cands[k - 1]["center"]}))))
    evo.run_parallel(jobs, 6)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if sys.argv[1] == "find":
        find(sys.argv[2], int(sys.argv[3]), sys.argv[4] if len(sys.argv) > 4 else "op60", sys.argv[5] if len(sys.argv) > 5 else "v1")
    else:
        write(sys.argv[2], int(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else 1)

    "N10c_测_清单": [("模板_BUILD.txt", ("var", "v6check", "BUILD")), ("模板_CHECK.txt", ("var", "v6check", "CHECK")), ("实发_op61.txt", ("run", "chk_op61_g6_h11"))],
    "N10d_测_盲评": [("模板.txt", ("var", "v6fix", "BLIND")), ("实发_op61.txt", ("run", "v6b_op61_g6_h11_vsb0"))],
    "N11_补漏": [("模板.txt", ("var", "v6human", "FIXLOST")), ("实发_op61.txt", ("run", "hl_op61_h13"))],
}


def get(src):
    kind = src[0]
    if kind == "file":
        return Path(src[1]).read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")
    if kind == "var":
        v = getattr(importlib.import_module(src[1]), src[2])
        return v if isinstance(v, str) else repr(v)
    if kind == "run":
        d = ROOT / "runs" / src[1]
        for n in ("input.txt", "prompt.txt"):
            if (d / n).exists():
                return (d / n).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        raise FileNotFoundError(d)
    if kind == "beadrun":
        return (BEAD / "runs" / src[1] / "S01.prompt.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    shutil.rmtree(OUT); OUT.mkdir(parents=True)
    for node, files in SPEC.items():
        (OUT / node).mkdir()
        for name, src in files:
            try:
                (OUT / node / name).write_text(get(src), encoding="utf-8")
                print("ok ", node, name)
            except Exception as e:
                print("MISS", node, name, repr(e)[:120])

FILE D:\musicclaude\_bead_op61_20261020\sat.py 
"""Op.61 copy of _bead_saturation_20261018/sat.py; only the production stages gen_b,rev1_b,rev2_b,final_b,piece_b,final2_b are used.
Op.60 semantic-saturation experiment (DAG A) — all model work via local gpt3 (GPT Web, GPT-6 Thinking / max).

Tasks per segment (cached under runs/<stage>/<seg>.*; a task with parsed.json is never redone):
  gen    fresh   ranked marginal-value ladder from Pool + 24 A
  rev1   fresh   clean reviewer lists the segment's experiences from 24 A only (sees no candidate)
  rev2   resume rev1 session: reviewer scores the anonymised ladder k by k
  final  resume gen session: generator answers the review and writes the final STRUCT
  prod   fresh   one-shot production candidate (Pool + A -> STRUCT, no ladder, no review)
  nopool fresh   one-shot without Pool (ablation, subset)
  cmp    fresh   blind comparison of final / prod / nopool
Scheduler: dependency-aware worker pool; concurrency read live from runs/parallel.txt.
A call that failed after submission is marked 'uncertain' and never resubmitted automatically.

  python sat.py run --stages gen,rev1,rev2,final,prod,cmp --segs all
  python sat.py status
"""
import argparse, hashlib, json, random, re, subprocess, sys, threading, time, zipfile
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
GPT3 = "D:/musicclaude/gpt2agent-safechain/bin/gpt3.cmd"
GPT3_RUNS = Path("D:/musicclaude/gpt2agent-safechain/runs/gpt3")
RUNS = ROOT / "runs"
INP = ROOT / "inputs"
STRUCT = json.loads((INP / "structure.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
SEGS = {f"S{i+1:02d}": (mm, lab) for i, (mm, lab) in enumerate(STRUCT["frozen_segments"])}
A = {p.stem: p.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig") for p in sorted((INP / "A").glob("*.md"))}
assert len(A) == 24 and not any(x in k.lower() for k in A for x in ("muse", "opus", "sonnet")), "need exactly the 24 non-Muse, non-Claude A sources"
class _Pool(dict):
    def __missing__(self, s):
        self[s] = json.loads((ROOT / "runs" / "pool_v2" / "pool" / f"{s}.parsed.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")); return self[s]
POOL = _Pool()  # lazy: rev1 does not need the pool
SEC_PER_MEASURE = 2.7  # ~13 min / 288 measures; rough attention budget only
NOPOOL_SEGS = []
MIN_GAP = 6.0


def mm(seg):
    return SEGS[seg][0].replace("-", "–")


def nmeas(seg):
    a, b = map(int, SEGS[seg][0].split("-")); return b - a + 1


SEGLIST = "；".join(f"{s} 第{mm(s)}小节 {lab}" for s, (_, lab) in SEGS.items())


def norm(s):
    return re.sub(r"[\s\*`“”\"'‘’「」『』《》，。、；：！？,.;:!?（）()\-—–…·]", "", s or "")


NA = {k: norm(v) for k, v in A.items()}


def quote_ok(src, q):
    q = norm(q); return bool(q) and src in NA and q in NA[src]


def sources_zip():
    z = RUNS / "sources.zip"
    if not z.exists():
        RUNS.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with zipfile.ZipFile(z, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as f:
            for k, v in A.items():
                f.writestr(f"A/{k}.md", v)
    return z


def tpl(name):
    return (ROOT / "prompts" / f"{name}.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")


def fill(t, seg, **kw):
    for k, v in kw.items():
        t = t.replace("{" + k + "}", v)
    return (t.replace("{SEG}", seg).replace("{MM}", mm(seg)).replace("{LABEL}", SEGS[seg][1])
             .replace("{SEGLIST}", SEGLIST).replace("{SEC}", str(round(nmeas(seg) * SEC_PER_MEASURE))))


def render_pool(seg):
    out = []
    for it in POOL[seg]["items"]:
        rr = it["src_range"]; ms = it["measures"]
        loc = f"原稿范围 {rr} → 判断 {ms}" if rr.replace("mm. ", "") != ms else f"{ms}"
        out.append(f"- {it['id']}｜{it['src']}｜{loc}｜{it['type']}｜原话「{it['quote']}」｜复述：{it['experience']}")
    return "\n".join(out)


# ---------------- gpt3 ----------------
_gap_lock = threading.Lock(); _last = [0.0]
LOG = RUNS / "calls.jsonl"; _log_lock = threading.Lock()


def log(rec):
    with _log_lock:
        with LOG.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(json.dumps(rec, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")


def call(stage, seg, prompt_text, attach=(), session=None):
    out = RUNS / stage; out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    pf = out / f"{seg}.prompt.txt"; pf.write_text(prompt_text, encoding="utf-8")
    assert len(prompt_text) <= 65000, f"{stage}/{seg} prompt too long: {len(prompt_text)}"
    for attempt in range(2):
        with _gap_lock:
            w = MIN_GAP - (time.time() - _last[0])
            if w > 0:
                time.sleep(w)
            _last[0] = time.time()
        cmd = [GPT3] + (["resume", session] if session else ["ask"]) + ["--file", str(pf), "--json"]
        for a in attach:
            cmd += ["--attach", str(a)]
        t0 = time.time(); started = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S")
        cp = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True)
        dt = time.time() - t0
        (out / f"{seg}.stdout.bin").write_bytes(cp.stdout); (out / f"{seg}.stderr.bin").write_bytes(cp.stderr)
        try:
            j = json.loads(cp.stdout.decode("utf-8"))
        except Exception:
            j = {}
        md = j.get("metadata") or {}
        job = md.get("job_directory")
        if not job:
            m = re.search(rb'"job_directory":\s*"((?:[^"\\]|\\.)*)"', cp.stdout + cp.stderr)
            job = json.loads(b'"' + m.group(1) + b'"') if m else None
        ok = cp.returncode == 0 and j.get("completed") is True and j.get("response")
        rec = {"stage": stage, "seg": seg, "attempt": attempt, "started": started, "seconds": round(dt),
               "rc": cp.returncode, "completed": bool(ok), "model": md.get("model"), "effort": md.get("thinking_effort"),
               "temporary": md.get("temporary"), "unpersonalized": md.get("unpersonalized"),
               "policy_verified": md.get("generation_policy_verified"), "resume": bool(session),
               "session_handle": md.get("session_handle"), "job": job,
               "prompt_chars": len(prompt_text), "prompt_sha256": hashlib.sha256(prompt_text.encode()).hexdigest()}
        if ok:
            if md.get("model") != "gpt-6-thinking" or md.get("thinking_effort") != "max" or not md.get("temporary") or not md.get("unpersonalized"):
                rec["completed"] = False; rec["why"] = "policy mismatch"; log(rec)
                print(f"[{stage}/{seg}] POLICY MISMATCH {md.get('model')}/{md.get('thinking_effort')}", flush=True)
                return None
            resp = j["response"]
            rec["response_chars"] = len(resp); log(rec)
            (out / f"{seg}.response.txt").write_text(resp, encoding="utf-8")
            (out / f"{seg}.meta.json").write_text(json.dumps(rec, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
            print(f"[{stage}/{seg}] ok {dt:.0f}s {len(resp)} chars", flush=True)
            return resp, md.get("session_handle")
        if b"gpt3_session_expired" in cp.stderr:  # refused locally before any request: not submitted, retry is useless
            rec["why"] = "session expired"; log(rec)
            print(f"[{stage}/{seg}] SESSION EXPIRED (not submitted)", flush=True)
            return None
        if not job:  # gpt3 failure output often omits job_directory: find our job by prompt text
            cands = [d for d in GPT3_RUNS.glob("pro-bridge-*") if d.stat().st_mtime >= t0 - 5 and (d / "prompt.txt").exists()
                     and (d / "prompt.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig").strip() == prompt_text.strip()]
            job = str(max(cands, key=lambda d: d.stat().st_mtime)) if cands else None
            rec["job"] = job
        # unknown job => treat as possibly submitted (never risk a duplicate submission)
        submitted = (not job) or (Path(job) / "submitted.json").exists()
        rec["submitted"] = submitted
        rec["stderr_tail"] = cp.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace")[-600:]
        log(rec)
        print(f"[{stage}/{seg}] FAILED rc={cp.returncode} {dt:.0f}s submitted={submitted}", flush=True)
        if submitted:
            (out / f"{seg}.uncertain.json").write_text(json.dumps(rec, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
            return None
        time.sleep(45)
    return None


def parse_json(resp):
    m = re.findall(r"```json\s*(.*?)```", resp, re.S)
    txt = m[-1] if m else resp
    try:
        return json.loads(txt)
    except Exception:
        s = txt[txt.find("{"): txt.rfind("}") + 1]
        return json.loads(s)


def load(stage, seg):
    p = RUNS / stage / f"{seg}.parsed.json"
    return json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if p.exists() else None


def handle(stage, seg):
    p = RUNS / stage / f"{seg}.meta.json"
    return json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("session_handle") if p.exists() else None


def save(stage, seg, d):
    (RUNS / stage / f"{seg}.parsed.json").write_text(json.dumps(d, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")


def verify_quotes(d):
    """Mark every {src, quote} in the structure with quote_verified."""
    n = bad = 0
    def walk(x):
        nonlocal n, bad
        if isinstance(x, dict):
            if "src" in x and "quote" in x and isinstance(x.get("quote"), str):
                x["quote_verified"] = quote_ok(x["src"], x["quote"]); n += 1; bad += not x["quote_verified"]
            for v in x.values():
                walk(v)
        elif isinstance(x, list):
            for v in x:
                walk(v)
    walk(d)
    return n, bad


def finish(stage, seg, r):
    if not r:
        return False
    resp, _ = r
    try:
        d = parse_json(resp)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[{stage}/{seg}] PARSE ERROR {e}", flush=True)
        (RUNS / stage / f"{seg}.parse_error.txt").write_text(str(e), encoding="utf-8")
        return False
    n, bad = verify_quotes(d)
    d["_quotes"] = {"n": n, "unverified": bad}
    save(stage, seg, d)
    return True


# ---------------- renderers ----------------
def render_ladder(seg, st="gen"):
    g = load(st, seg); out = []
    for f in g["ladder"]:
        rel = "；".join(f"{r.get('type')}（对 {','.join(r.get('to') or []) or '—'}）" for r in f.get("relation", []))
        src = "\n".join(f"  - {s.get('src')}：「{s.get('quote')}」" for s in f.get("sources", []))
        alt = "\n".join(f"  - {s.get('src')}：「{s.get('quote')}」" for s in f.get("also_expressed_by", []) or [])
        blk = (f"### 第 {f['k']} 个侧面（{f['id']}）\n小节：{f.get('measures')}（{f.get('placement')}）\n"
               f"写作者标注的关系：{rel or '—'}\n")
        if f.get("performance_condition"):
            blk += f"演奏条件：{f['performance_condition']}\n"
        blk += f"正文：{f['text']}\n依据原话：\n{src}\n"
        if alt:
            blk += f"并入的其他原稿说法：\n{alt}\n"
        out.append(blk)
    return "\n".join(out)


def render_review(seg, s1="rev1", s2="rev2"):
    r2 = {k: v for k, v in load(s2, seg).items() if not k.startswith("_")}
    r1 = load(s1, seg)
    ex = {e["id"]: e for e in r1.get("experiences", [])}
    ids = sorted({m.get("id") for m in r2.get("missing", []) if m.get("id") in ex})
    ref = [{"id": i, "what": ex[i].get("what"), "best_quote": ex[i].get("best_quote"), "measures": ex[i].get("measures"),
            "sources": [s.get("src") for s in ex[i].get("sources", [])]} for i in ids]
    txt = "```json\n" + json.dumps(r2, ensure_ascii=False, indent=0) + "\n```\n"
    txt += "\n审稿意见中的 E 编号是审稿人自己从原稿列出的体验编号。"
    if ref:
        txt += "审稿人认为遗漏的体验详情：\n```json\n" + json.dumps(ref, ensure_ascii=False, indent=0) + "\n```\n"
    return txt


def render_struct(d):
    beads = "\n".join(f"- [{b.get('role')}｜{b.get('measures')}] {b.get('text')}" for b in d.get("beads", []))
    return f"### 标题\n{d.get('title')}\n\n### 读者正文\n{d.get('reader')}\n\n### 结构化体验条目\n{beads}\n"


# ---------------- tasks ----------------
def t_gen(seg):
    p = fill(tpl("gen_v1"), seg, POOL=render_pool(seg), NPOOL=str(len(POOL[seg]["items"])))
    return finish("gen", seg, call("gen", seg, p, [sources_zip()]))


def t_rev1(seg):
    return finish("rev1", seg, call("rev1", seg, fill(tpl("rev1_v1"), seg), [sources_zip()]))


def t_rev2(seg):
    h = handle("rev1", seg)
    return finish("rev2", seg, call("rev2", seg, fill(tpl("rev2_v1"), seg, LADDER=render_ladder(seg)), session=h))


def t_final(seg):
    spec = fill(tpl("struct_spec_v1"), seg)
    p = fill(tpl("final_v1"), seg, STRUCT_SPEC=spec, REVIEW=render_review(seg))
    return finish("final", seg, call("final", seg, p, session=handle("gen", seg)))


def t_prod(seg, with_pool=True, ver="prod_v1", st=None):
    spec = fill(tpl("struct_spec_v1"), seg)
    if with_pool:
        note = "- 文末的 Pool：此前从这 24 份原稿中为本段登记的原文条目。原话已逐字核对；`→` 后的位置是登记者的判断，可能有误，有疑问请回原稿核对上下文。Pool 只是检索索引，不是必须保留的清单。"
        block = f"\n## Pool（{seg}，共 {len(POOL[seg]['items'])} 条）\n\n" + render_pool(seg)
    else:
        note = "- 请自己从 24 份原稿中找出关于本段音乐的内容；原稿范围宽或跨段时，按原稿描述的音乐事件判断它是否真落在本段。"
        block = ""
    chars = str(max(100, round(nmeas(seg) * SEC_PER_MEASURE * 5 / 10) * 10))
    p = fill(tpl(ver), seg, STRUCT_SPEC=spec, POOL_NOTE=note, POOL_BLOCK=block, CHARS=chars)
    st = st or ("prod" if with_pool else "nopool")
    return finish(st, seg, call(st, seg, p, [sources_zip()]))


def t_cmp(seg, tg=None, out="cmp"):
    tg = tg or (["final", "prod"] + (["nopool"] if seg in NOPOOL_SEGS else []))
    labels = ["甲", "乙", "丙"][:len(tg)]
    order = tg[:]; random.Random(f"{out}-{seg}").shuffle(order)
    mapping = dict(zip(labels, order))
    vs = "\n".join(f"## 版本{l}\n\n{render_struct(load(mapping[l], seg))}" for l in labels)
    ok = finish(out, seg, call(out, seg, fill(tpl("cmp_v1"), seg, VERSIONS=vs), [sources_zip()]))
    if ok:
        d = load(out, seg); d["_mapping"] = mapping; save(out, seg, d)
    return ok


def t_piece(stage="final", out="piece"):
    blocks = []
    for s in SEGS:
        d = load(stage, s)
        blocks.append(f"## {s}（第 {mm(s)} 小节，{SEGS[s][1]}）\n\n{render_struct(d)}")
    p = tpl("piece_v1").replace("{ALL}", "\n".join(blocks))
    return finish(out, "ALL", call(out, "ALL", p, [sources_zip()]))


def piece_for(seg, src="piece"):
    d = load(src, "ALL") or {}
    ps = [x for x in d.get("per_segment", []) if x.get("segment") == seg]
    rt = [x for x in d.get("repeated_templates", []) if seg in (x.get("segments") or [])]
    fx = [x for x in d.get("fixes", []) if x.get("segment") == seg]
    ci = [x for x in d.get("continuity_issues", []) if seg in json.dumps(x, ensure_ascii=False)]
    return {"per_segment": ps, "repeated_templates": rt, "fixes": fx, "continuity_issues": ci}


def needs_fix(seg, src="piece"):
    p = piece_for(seg, src)
    own = (p["per_segment"] or [{}])[0].get("own_experience")
    return bool(p["fixes"]) or own in ("部分", "否") or any(x.get("is_problem") for x in p["repeated_templates"])


def t_fix(seg, src="piece", gen="gen", out="final2"):
    spec = fill(tpl("struct_spec_v1"), seg)
    txt = "```json\n" + json.dumps(piece_for(seg, src), ensure_ascii=False, indent=0) + "\n```"
    p = fill(tpl("fix_v1"), seg, STRUCT_SPEC=spec, PIECE=txt)
    return finish(out, seg, call(out, seg, p, session=handle(gen, seg)))


def t_trim(seg):
    d = load("cmp2", seg); inv = {v: k for k, v in d["_mapping"].items()}
    v = d["versions"][inv["final"]]
    note = {"attention_fit": v.get("attention_fit"), "attention_note": v.get("attention_note"), "errors": v.get("errors", [])}
    txt = "```json\n" + json.dumps(note, ensure_ascii=False, indent=0) + "\n```"
    p = fill(tpl("trim_v1"), seg, STRUCT_SPEC=fill(tpl("struct_spec_v1"), seg), NOTE=txt)
    return finish("final3", seg, call("final3", seg, p, session=handle("gen", seg)))


def t_cmp3(seg):
    best = "final3"
    d = load("cmp2", seg); m = d["_mapping"]; winner = [m[x] for x in d["ranking"] if m[x] != "final"][0]
    return t_cmp(seg, [best, winner, "final"], "cmp3")


# ---- Pool (bead_chain pool_v2 prompt, same fill/parse/quote check as chain.st_pool; run here so one process paces all gpt3 calls) ----
def t_pool(seg):
    st = "pool_v2/pool"
    r = call(st, seg, fill(tpl("pool_v2"), seg), [sources_zip()])
    if not r:
        return False
    try:
        d = parse_json(r[0])
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[pool/{seg}] PARSE ERROR {e}", flush=True)
        (RUNS / st / f"{seg}.parse_error.txt").write_text(str(e), encoding="utf-8"); return False
    for it in d.get("items", []):
        it["quote_verified"] = quote_ok(it.get("src", ""), it.get("quote", ""))
    save(st, seg, d)
    return True


# ---- DAG B validation: the proposed production chain run fresh (separate sessions, *_b stages) ----
def t_gen_b(seg):
    p = fill(tpl("gen_v1"), seg, POOL=render_pool(seg), NPOOL=str(len(POOL[seg]["items"])))
    return finish("gen_b", seg, call("gen_b", seg, p, [sources_zip()]))


def t_rev1_b(seg):
    return finish("rev1_b", seg, call("rev1_b", seg, fill(tpl("rev1_v1"), seg), [sources_zip()]))


def t_rev2_b(seg):
    lad = render_ladder(seg, "gen_b")
    return finish("rev2_b", seg, call("rev2_b", seg, fill(tpl("rev2_v1"), seg, LADDER=lad), session=handle("rev1_b", seg)))


def t_final_b(seg):
    spec = fill(tpl("struct_spec_v1"), seg)
    p = fill(tpl("final_v2"), seg, STRUCT_SPEC=spec, REVIEW=render_review(seg, "rev1_b", "rev2_b"))
    return finish("final_b", seg, call("final_b", seg, p, session=handle("gen_b", seg)))


# ---- selection (DAG A only): per-segment winner of the last blind 3-way comparison ----
CHOSEN = {"S01": "final_b", "S02": "final_b", "S03": "final", "S04": "final", "S05": "final_b", "S06": "final_b",
          "S07": "final", "S08": "final3", "S09": "final_b", "S10": "final_b", "S11": "final", "S12": "final_b",
          "S13": "final_b", "S14": "final_b"}
GEN_OF = {"final": "gen", "final2": "gen", "final3": "gen", "final_b": "gen_b", "final2_b": "gen_b"}


def materialize_chosen():
    (RUNS / "chosen").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for s, st in CHOSEN.items():
        d = dict(load(st, s)); d["_from"] = st
        save("chosen", s, d)


def t_fix_c(seg):
    return t_fix(seg, "piece_c", GEN_OF[CHOSEN[seg]], "final2_c")


def t_fix_fresh(seg, src="piece_b", base="final_b", out="final2_b"):
    """Op.61: the gen_b sessions passed gpt3's 6 h lifetime, so the fix runs in a fresh session that is given
    the segment's own final_b STRUCT verbatim (minus script marks) plus the same 24 sources."""
    prev = {k: v for k, v in load(base, seg).items() if not k.startswith("_")}
    def strip(x):
        if isinstance(x, dict):
            return {k: strip(v) for k, v in x.items() if k not in ("quote_verified", "quote_found_in")}
        if isinstance(x, list):
            return [strip(v) for v in x]
        return x
    head = (f"下面是你此前为肖邦《幻想波兰舞曲》Op.61 的 {seg}（第 {mm(seg)} 小节，{SEGS[seg][1]}）写出的最终 Bead / STRUCT。"
            "附件 `sources.zip` 的 `A/` 是写作时依据的 24 份历史聆听原稿（文件名即来源 ID）。\n\n"
            "```json\n" + json.dumps(strip(prev), ensure_ascii=False, indent=0) + "\n```\n\n")
    spec = fill(tpl("struct_spec_v1"), seg)
    txt = "```json\n" + json.dumps(piece_for(seg, src), ensure_ascii=False, indent=0) + "\n```"
    p = head + fill(tpl("fix_v1"), seg, STRUCT_SPEC=spec, PIECE=txt)
    return finish(out, seg, call(out, seg, p, [sources_zip()]))


TASKS = {
    "gen": (t_gen, lambda s: []), "rev1": (t_rev1, lambda s: []),
    "rev2": (t_rev2, lambda s: [("gen", s), ("rev1", s)]),
    "final": (t_final, lambda s: [("rev2", s)]),
    "prod": (lambda s: t_prod(s, True), lambda s: []),
    "nopool": (lambda s: t_prod(s, False), lambda s: []),
    "prod2": (lambda s: t_prod(s, True, "prod_v2", "prod2"), lambda s: []),
    "cmp2": (lambda s: t_cmp(s, ["final", "prod", "prod2"], "cmp2"), lambda s: [("final", s), ("prod", s), ("prod2", s)]),
    "final3": (t_trim, lambda s: [("cmp2", s)]),
    "cmp3": (t_cmp3, lambda s: [("final3", s)]),
    "pool": (t_pool, lambda s: []),
    "gen_b": (t_gen_b, lambda s: [("pool", s)]), "rev1_b": (t_rev1_b, lambda s: []),
    "rev2_b": (t_rev2_b, lambda s: [("gen_b", s), ("rev1_b", s)]),
    "final_b": (t_final_b, lambda s: [("rev2_b", s)]),
    "cmpb": (lambda s: t_cmp(s, ["final_b", "final", "prod"], "cmpb"), lambda s: [("final_b", s)]),
    "final2_b": (lambda s: t_fix_fresh(s), lambda s: [("final_b", s)]),
    "final2_c": (t_fix_c, lambda s: []),
    "final2": (t_fix, lambda s: [("final", s)]),
    "cmp": (t_cmp, lambda s: [("final", s), ("prod", s)] + ([("nopool", s)] if s in NOPOOL_SEGS else [])),
}
PRIORITY = ["pool", "final_b", "rev2_b", "cmpb", "gen_b", "rev1_b", "final3", "cmp3", "final2", "prod2", "cmp2", "final", "rev2", "gen", "rev1", "cmp", "prod", "nopool"]


def done(stage, seg):
    if stage == "pool":
        return (RUNS / "pool_v2" / "pool" / f"{seg}.parsed.json").exists()
    return (RUNS / stage / f"{seg}.parsed.json").exists()


def blocked(stage, seg):
    d = RUNS / ("pool_v2/pool" if stage == "pool" else stage)
    return (d / f"{seg}.uncertain.json").exists() or (d / f"{seg}.parse_error.txt").exists()


def parallel():
    try:
        return int((RUNS / "parallel.txt").read_text().strip())
    except Exception:
        return 6


def run(stages, segs):
    pending = {(st, s) for st in stages for s in segs if not (st == "nopool" and s not in NOPOOL_SEGS)}
    pending = {t for t in pending if not done(*t)}
    running, failed = set(), set()
    lock = threading.Lock()

    def worker(t):
        ok = False
        try:
            ok = TASKS[t[0]][0](t[1])
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[{t[0]}/{t[1]}] EXC {e!r}", flush=True)
        with lock:
            running.discard(t)
            if not ok:
                failed.add(t)

    while True:
        with lock:
            for t in list(pending):
                if blocked(*t):
                    pending.discard(t); failed.add(t)
            ready = [t for t in pending if all(done(*d) for d in TASKS[t[0]][1](t[1]))]
            dead = [t for t in pending if any(d in failed for d in TASKS[t[0]][1](t[1]))]
            for t in dead:
                pending.discard(t); failed.add(t)
            ready.sort(key=lambda t: (PRIORITY.index(t[0]) if t[0] in PRIORITY else 0, t[1]))
            slots = parallel() - len(running)
            launch = ready[:max(0, slots)]
            for t in launch:
                pending.discard(t); running.add(t)
            if not pending and not running:
                break
        for t in launch:
            threading.Thread(target=worker, args=(t,), daemon=True).start()
            time.sleep(0.2)
        time.sleep(3)
    print("DONE; failed:", sorted(failed), flush=True)


def status():
    for st in TASKS:
        d = RUNS / st
        ok = sorted(p.name[:3] for p in d.glob("S*.parsed.json")) if d.exists() else []
        unc = sorted(p.name[:3] for p in d.glob("S*.uncertain.json")) if d.exists() else []
        print(f"{st:7s} done {len(ok):2d} {','.join(ok)}" + (f"  UNCERTAIN {unc}" if unc else ""))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(); ap.add_argument("cmd"); ap.add_argument("--stages", default="gen,rev1,rev2,final,prod,nopool,cmp")
    ap.add_argument("--segs", default="all"); a = ap.parse_args()
    segs = list(SEGS) if a.segs == "all" else a.segs.split(",")
    if a.cmd == "fixlist":
        print(",".join(s for s in SEGS if needs_fix(s)))
    elif a.cmd == "piece":
        if a.stages == "chosen":
            materialize_chosen()
        t_piece(a.stages, {"final": "piece", "final_b": "piece_b", "chosen": "piece_c"}.get(a.stages, "piece_" + a.stages))
    elif a.cmd == "run":
        run(a.stages.split(","), segs)
    else:
        status()


FILE D:\musicclaude\_bead_op61_20261020\finish.py 
"""Remaining production steps, run as one detached process (survives app restarts; every step is cached/resumable).
S10 gen_b -> rev2_b -> final_b ; piece_b over all 13 final_b ; final2_b for segments the piece check flags ; build."""
import subprocess, sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
import sat  # noqa: E402


def main():
    sat.run(["pool", "gen_b", "rev1_b", "rev2_b", "final_b"], list(sat.SEGS))
    missing = [s for s in sat.SEGS if not sat.done("final_b", s)]
    if missing:
        print("STOP: final_b missing", missing, flush=True); return
    if not sat.load("piece_b", "ALL"):
        if sat.blocked("piece_b", "ALL"):
            print("STOP: piece_b uncertain/parse error", flush=True); return
        sat.t_piece("final_b", "piece_b")
    if not sat.load("piece_b", "ALL"):
        print("STOP: piece_b failed", flush=True); return
    fl = [s for s in sat.SEGS if sat.needs_fix(s, "piece_b")]
    print("piece_b flags:", fl, flush=True)
    (ROOT / "runs" / "piece_b_flags.txt").write_text(",".join(fl), encoding="utf-8")
    if fl:
        sat.run(["final2_b"], fl)
    subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT / "build_op61.py")], cwd=ROOT)
    print("FINISHED", flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE D:\musicclaude\_bead_op61_20261020\prompts\struct_spec_v1.md 
## 最终 Bead / STRUCT 的要求

这是经过选择的体验内容，不是研究摘要。数量由本段音乐、表达之间的重叠和新增内容的边际价值决定；不规定条数，也不规定上限。

- `beads`：最终保留的每一种体验。
  - `text`：给普通听者读的自然中文，保留原稿中最鲜活的具体画面、动作、触感和说法；允许小幅组织和拼接相容内容，但不全面重写、不洗成抽象解释。
  - `role`：`主体验`／`另一侧面`（与其他体验可同时成立）／`另一种理解`（与某体验是不同听法）／`段内变化`（音乐内部的先后变化）／`演奏条件`（只在某种演奏处理下成立）。
  - `measures`、`placement`（`stated`／`inferred`／`segment_wide`，不能比证据更精确）。
  - `performance_condition`：演奏条件，没有就写 null。
  - `sources`：逐字原话（每条不超过 60 字）＋来源 ID。
  - `also_expressed_by`：并入此体验的其他鲜活原话（逐字＋来源 ID），供以后呈现时替换使用；没有可为空。
  - `vivid_lines`：可以直接上屏的原稿原句（逐字），没有可为空。
- `relations`：只写真实存在的关系：`可同时成立`／`另一种理解`／`段内先后`／`重叠但新增`，并写一句说明。没有就留空，不必为了格式整齐每类都出现。
- `timeline`：只有本段内部确实有先后变化时才写（每步：小节、音乐事件、相关 bead）；否则为空数组。
- `reader`：给听者的连续自然中文正文，可以只选 beads 中适合当前呈现的内容。不同来源的不同理解要让读者看得出是不同的听法，不要串成同一个人的经历或因果链。不要套用全曲通用的人物故事。
- `reader_uses`：正文用到的 bead id。
- `title`：本段一个短标题（不超过 16 字），来自本段真正的体验。
- `stop_reason`：为什么停在这些体验，不再增加。
- `not_kept`：值得记录的、未保留的重要候选及原因（简短；只列有判断价值的几条）。
- `internal_note`：内部理解（不上屏），没有就留空。

戏剧性、肯定、单一故事的写法本身不是缺点；问题只在于把没有证据的关系拼起来，或用抽象概括替代具体体验。

只输出一个 JSON 代码块，**直接写在回答正文里**，不要生成文件或下载链接，不要联网搜索。

```json
{
  "segment": "{SEG}",
  "title": "",
  "beads": [
    {"id": "B1", "role": "主体验", "text": "", "measures": "", "placement": "stated",
     "performance_condition": null,
     "sources": [{"src": "SRC-xxx", "quote": ""}],
     "also_expressed_by": [{"src": "SRC-xxx", "quote": ""}],
     "vivid_lines": [{"src": "SRC-xxx", "quote": ""}]}
  ],
  "relations": [{"a": "B1", "b": "B2", "type": "可同时成立", "note": ""}],
  "timeline": [],
  "reader": "",
  "reader_uses": ["B1"],
  "stop_reason": "",
  "not_kept": [{"what": "", "why": ""}],
  "review_responses": [{"issue": "", "decision": "采纳|部分采纳|不采纳", "why": ""}],
  "internal_note": ""
}
```
（`review_responses` 只在有审稿意见时填写，否则为空数组。）

+# ---- Pool (bead_chain pool_v2 prompt, same fill/parse/quote check as chain.st_pool; run here so one process paces all gpt3 calls) ----

## 任务：为本段建立完整的原稿账本（Pool）

逐份读完全部 24 份原稿，把**每一处说到本段音乐的内容**登记为一条 item。这一步只登记、不评判好坏、不跨稿合并：

1. **一处一条**：同一份原稿里，每个可以单独成立的感受、画面、动因、身体感觉、先后变化、演奏差异各登记一条；不同原稿说同一件事也各自登记。宁可细，不要漏。
2. **范围判断**：原稿给出的范围（`src_range`）照实记录。若原稿范围比本段宽或跨段，判断这句话真正落在哪几个小节（`measures`），并在 `range_reason` 写依据（原稿明说的小节、音乐事件的位置、上下文）。原稿小节号明显按错误编号时，按它描述的音乐事件对应到真实位置，并说明。
3. **排除登记**：原稿范围与本段相交、但这句话实际属于相邻段落的，放进 `excluded`，写明属于哪里。完全不涉及本段的内容不用登记。
4. **原话**：每条附原稿原话 `quote`，必须逐字照抄、不超过 60 字、不改标点以外的任何字。
5. **复述**：`experience` 用一两句平实中文说清这条在说什么体验（不要加入原稿没有的人物、场景或因果）。
6. **每份原稿都要出现在 `sources_checked`**，写它为本段登记了几条；没有就写 0。

sat scheduler tail:
def done(stage, seg):
    if stage == "pool":
        return (RUNS / "pool_v2" / "pool" / f"{seg}.parsed.json").exists()
    return (RUNS / stage / f"{seg}.parsed.json").exists()


def blocked(stage, seg):
    d = RUNS / ("pool_v2/pool" if stage == "pool" else stage)
    return (d / f"{seg}.uncertain.json").exists() or (d / f"{seg}.parse_error.txt").exists()


def parallel():
    try:
        return int((RUNS / "parallel.txt").read_text().strip())
    except Exception:
        return 6


def run(stages, segs):
    pending = {(st, s) for st in stages for s in segs if not (st == "nopool" and s not in NOPOOL_SEGS)}
    pending = {t for t in pending if not done(*t)}
    running, failed = set(), set()
    lock = threading.Lock()

    def worker(t):
        ok = False
        try:
            ok = TASKS[t[0]][0](t[1])
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[{t[0]}/{t[1]}] EXC {e!r}", flush=True)
        with lock:
            running.discard(t)
            if not ok:
                failed.add(t)

    while True:
        with lock:
            for t in list(pending):
                if blocked(*t):
                    pending.discard(t); failed.add(t)
            ready = [t for t in pending if all(done(*d) for d in TASKS[t[0]][1](t[1]))]
            dead = [t for t in pending if any(d in failed for d in TASKS[t[0]][1](t[1]))]
            for t in dead:
                pending.discard(t); failed.add(t)
            ready.sort(key=lambda t: (PRIORITY.index(t[0]) if t[0] in PRIORITY else 0, t[1]))
            slots = parallel() - len(running)
            launch = ready[:max(0, slots)]
            for t in launch:
                pending.discard(t); running.add(t)
            if not pending and not running:
                break
        for t in launch:
            threading.Thread(target=worker, args=(t,), daemon=True).start()
            time.sleep(0.2)
        time.sleep(3)
    print("DONE; failed:", sorted(failed), flush=True)

写这首曲子时（1845–1846年），肖邦的肺结核已经恶化，与乔治·桑的关系正在走向决裂。但他没有写一首撕心裂肺的控诉，而是把一生对温存、肉体欢愉、遗憾和超越的全部理解，融进了一片流动的水波中。

以下是沉浸其中时，你会经历的心灵轨迹与状态转换：

* **心理状态：卸下戒备的疲惫感，夹杂着“偷来的安宁”。**
* **具体体验：**
  * **mm. 1–4：** 开头那个从低音沉沉坠下、又缓缓升起的琶音，就像一脚踏上小船时，木板微晃、水波在脚下荡开的失重感。陆地上的噪音、人际的琐碎与身体的沉重，在这一推

# Build jobs from versioned prompt templates and run them via runner.
import json, sys, os
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(__file__))
from runner import run_jobs, fill

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
WORKS = {
    "op52": "肖邦《第四叙事曲》f小调 Op.52",
    "op60": "肖邦《船歌》升F大调 Op.60",
    "op61": "肖邦《幻想波兰舞曲》降A大调 Op.61",
}

# internal-weight branches on agy must not touch the network
NET_TOOLS = ["search_web", "read_url_content", "browser_subagent", "open_browser_url", "read_browser_page",
             "call_mcp_tool", "run_command"]

def job(branch, version, model_key, work_key, sample=1, extra=None, **fillkv):
    tpl_rel = f"prompts/versions/{version}.txt"
    prompt = fill((ROOT / tpl_rel).read_text(encoding="utf-8"), WORK=WORKS[work_key], **fillkv)
    j = {"job_id": f"{branch}__{version}__{model_key}__{work_key}__s{sample}", "branch": branch,
         "model_key": model_key, "prompt": prompt, "prompt_version": version,
         "prompt_template": tpl_rel, "work_key": work_key, "work_name": WORKS[work_key],
         "sample": sample, "extra": extra or {}}
    if branch.endswith("-int") and not model_key.endswith("_web") and model_key != "luna":
        # every internal-weight task is offline: agy keys, luna_noweb and muse alike.
        # (plain luna is the online model; it never runs -int branches.)
        j["forbid_tools"] = list(NET_TOOLS)
        j["max_attempts"] = 10 if model_key in ("muse", "muse_exec") else 6
    return j

def run(run_id, jobs, workers=12, timeout=3600):
    run_dir = ROOT / "runs" / run_id
    run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (run_dir / "jobs.json").write_text(json.dumps(
        [{k: v for k, v in j.items() if k != "prompt"} for j in jobs], ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    return run_jobs(run_dir, jobs, max_workers=workers, timeout=timeout)

+"""Offline work metadata shared by N02/N03/N04. No model imports or I/O at import time."""
+import json
+import os
+import re
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+
+
+def load(key, works_root=None):
+    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_-]+", key):
+        raise ValueError(f"invalid work key: {key!r}")
+    base = Path(works_root or os.environ.get("WORKS_ROOT") or ROOT / "works")
+    d = json.loads((base / key / "work.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    for field in ("composer", "title_zh", "tonality", "opus", "segmentation_source",
+                  "specificity_candidates", "specificity_right", "seconds_per_measure", "fix_session"):
+        if not d.get(field):
+            raise ValueError(f"{key}: missing {field}")
+    if d.get("key", key) != key:
+        raise ValueError(f"{key}: work.json key mismatch")
+    cs = d["specificity_candidates"]
+    if not isinstance(cs, list) or len(cs) < 2 or len(set(cs)) != len(cs) or d["specificity_right"] not in cs:
+        raise ValueError(f"{key}: candidates must be unique and contain specificity_right")
+    if d["fix_session"] not in ("fresh", "resume") or d["seconds_per_measure"] <= 0:
+        raise ValueError(f"{key}: invalid fix_session/seconds_per_measure")
+    d = dict(d, key=key, _dir=base / key)
+    return d
+
+
+def name(d, with_key=False):
+    return d["composer"] + d["title_zh"] + (d["tonality"] + " " if with_key else "") + d["opus"]
+
+
+def segmentation(d):
+    s = json.loads((d["_dir"] / "segmentation.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    rows = s["segments"]
+    end = 0
+    for i, row in enumerate(rows, 1):
+        if row["id"] != f"S{i:02d}" or row["start"] != end + 1 or row["end"] < row["start"]:
+            raise ValueError("segmentation must have consecutive Sxx ids and closed, contiguous ranges")
+        end = row["end"]
+    if not rows or end != s["total_measures"] or s["boundaries"] != [x["start"] for x in rows] + [end + 1]:
+        raise ValueError("segmentation boundaries/total_measures disagree")
+    return s
+
+
+def chinese_number(n):
+    digits = "零一二三四五六七八九"
+    if n < 10:
+        return digits[n]
+    if n < 100:
+        return (digits[n // 10] if n >= 20 else "") + "十" + (digits[n % 10] if n % 10 else "")
+    return str(n)
+
+
+def values(d, s=None):
+    s = s or segmentation(d)
+    n = chinese_number(len(s["segments"]))
+    source = d["segmentation_source"]
+    return {"WORK": name(d), "WORK_WITH_KEY": name(d, True), "COMPOSER": d["composer"],
+            "SEGMENTATION_SOURCE": source + "冻结" + n + "段",
+            "SEGMENTATION_LIST_SOURCE": source + "冻结的" + n + "段", "NSEG_ZH": n}
+
+
+def replace(text, params):
+    # One pass: never reinterpret placeholders appearing in quoted model/source text.
+    return re.sub(r"\{([A-Z_]+)\}", lambda m: str(params.get(m[1], m[0])), text)
+
+
+def render(text, d, s=None):
+    return replace(text, values(d, s))
+
+
+def source_dir(d):
+    return Path(d.get("a_sources") or d["_dir"] / "A")
+
+
+def selected(default=("op60", "op61"), work=None):
+    value = work or os.environ.get("WORK")
+    return value.split(",") if value else list(default)
+
+
+def write_prompts(directory, prompts):
+    """Exclusive output, so offline checks cannot overwrite historical evidence."""
+    out = Path(directory)
+    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    for filename, text in prompts.items():
+        with (out / filename).open("x", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as f:
+            f.write(text)

import work_meta

def configure(work=None):
    global ROOT, META, INP, SEGS, SHORT, A, NA
    META = work_meta.load(work or os.environ.get(\"WORK\", \"op61\"))
    sd = work_meta.segmentation(META)

+def template(name):
+    base = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "prompts"
+    return work_meta.render((base / f"{name}.param.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8"), META)
+
+
+def production_runtime(history_root=None):
+    """Reuse the production tasks and pacing/cache rules without changing sat.py."""
+    import importlib.util
+    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("_parameterized_bead_sat", Path(__file__).with_name("sat.py"))
+    rt = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+    spec.loader.exec_module(rt)
+    rt.ROOT = ROOT
+    rt.RUNS = Path(history_root) if history_root else ROOT / "runs"
+    rt.INP = INP
+    rt.SEGS = SEGS
+    rt.A = A; rt.NA = NA
+    rt.SEC_PER_MEASURE = META["seconds_per_measure"]
+    rt.SEGLIST = "；".join(f"{s} 第{rt.mm(s)}小节 {lab}" for s, (_, lab) in SEGS.items())
+    rt.tpl = template
+    rt.LOG = rt.RUNS / "calls.jsonl"
+
+    class Pool(dict):
+        def __missing__(self, seg):
+            self[seg] = json.loads((rt.RUNS / "pool_v2" / "pool" / f"{seg}.parsed.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+            return self[seg]
+
+    rt.POOL = Pool()
+
+    def fix_fresh(seg):
+        prev = {k: v for k, v in rt.load("final_b", seg).items() if not k.startswith("_")}
+
+        def strip(x):
+            if isinstance(x, dict):
+                return {k: strip(v) for k, v in x.items() if k not in ("quote_verified", "quote_found_in")}
+            if isinstance(x, list):
+                return [strip(v) for v in x]
+            return x
+
+        head = rt.fill(template("fix_fresh_head").replace("{PREVIOUS}", json.dumps(strip(prev), ensure_ascii=False, indent=0)), seg)
+        txt = "```json\n" + json.dumps(rt.piece_for(seg, "piece_b"), ensure_ascii=False, indent=0) + "\n```"
+        msg = head + rt.fill(template("fix_v1"), seg, STRUCT_SPEC=rt.fill(template("struct_spec_v1"), seg), PIECE=txt)
+        return rt.finish("final2_b", seg, rt.call("final2_b", seg, msg, [rt.sources_zip()]))
+
+    rt.TASKS["final2_b"] = ((fix_fresh if META["fix_session"] == "fresh" else
+                            lambda s: rt.t_fix(s, "piece_b", "gen_b", "final2_b")),
+                           lambda s: [("final_b", s)])
+    rt.parameterized_fix = rt.TASKS["final2_b"][0]
+    return rt
+
+
+def production(a):
+    rt = production_runtime(a.history_root)
+    segs = list(SEGS) if a.segs == "all" else a.segs.split(",")
+    if a.stage == "render":
+        if not a.render_dir:
+            raise ValueError("render requires --render-dir; --history-root must supply prior stage payloads")
+        prompts = {}
+
+        def capture(stage, seg, prompt_text, attach=(), session=None):
+            prompts[f"{stage.replace('/', '_')}.{seg}.txt"] = prompt_text
+            return None  # finish() makes no writes without a response
+
+        rt.call = capture
+        rt.sources_zip = lambda: rt.RUNS / "sources.zip"  # no archive creation during rendering
+        for s in segs:
+            rt.t_pool(s); rt.t_gen_b(s); rt.t_rev1_b(s); rt.t_rev2_b(s); rt.t_final_b(s)
+            prompts[f"struct_spec.{s}.txt"] = rt.fill(template("struct_spec_v1"), s)
+        rt.t_piece("final_b", "piece_b")
+        for s in segs:
+            rt.parameterized_fix(s)
+        work_meta.write_prompts(a.render_dir, prompts)
+        return
+    assert len(A) == 24 and not any(x in k.lower() for k in A for x in ("muse", "opus", "sonnet")), "need the 24 non-Muse, non-Claude A sources"
+    rt.run(["pool", "gen_b", "rev1_b", "rev2_b", "final_b"], segs)
+    if any(not rt.done("final_b", s) for s in SEGS):
+        raise RuntimeError("final_b incomplete: stop before whole-piece review")
+    if not rt.done("piece_b", "ALL"):
+        if rt.blocked("piece_b", "ALL") or not rt.t_piece("final_b", "piece_b"):
+            raise RuntimeError("piece_b blocked or failed")
+    flagged = [s for s in SEGS if rt.needs_fix(s, "piece_b")]
+    if flagged:
+        rt.run(["final2_b"], flagged)
+    if any(not rt.done("final2_b", s) for s in flagged):
+        raise RuntimeError("flagged fixes incomplete")
+    print("N03 complete:", rt.RUNS, "fixed:", flagged)
+
+

+"""Offline work metadata shared by N02/N03/N04. No model imports or I/O at import time."""
+import json
+import os
+import re
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+
+
+def load(key, works_root=None):
+    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_-]+", key):
+        raise ValueError(f"invalid work key: {key!r}")
+    base = Path(works_root or os.environ.get("WORKS_ROOT") or ROOT / "works")
+    d = json.loads((base / key / "work.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    for field in ("composer", "title_zh", "tonality", "opus", "segmentation_source",
+                  "specificity_candidates", "specificity_right", "seconds_per_measure", "fix_session"):
+        if not d.get(field):
+            raise ValueError(f"{key}: missing {field}")
+    if d.get("key", key) != key:
+        raise ValueError(f"{key}: work.json key mismatch")
+    cs = d["specificity_candidates"]
+    if not isinstance(cs, list) or len(cs) < 2 or len(set(cs)) != len(cs) or d["specificity_right"] not in cs:
+        raise ValueError(f"{key}: candidates must be unique and contain specificity_right")
+    if d["fix_session"] not in ("fresh", "resume") or d["seconds_per_measure"] <= 0:
+        raise ValueError(f"{key}: invalid fix_session/seconds_per_measure")
+    d = dict(d, key=key, _dir=base / key)
+    return d
+
+
+def name(d, with_key=False):
+    return d["composer"] + d["title_zh"] + (d["tonality"] + " " if with_key else "") + d["opus"]
+
+
+def segmentation(d):
+    s = json.loads((d["_dir"] / "segmentation.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    rows = s["segments"]
+    end = 0
+    for i, row in enumerate(rows, 1):
+        if row["id"] != f"S{i:02d}" or row["start"] != end + 1 or row["end"] < row["start"]:
+            raise ValueError("segmentation must have consecutive Sxx ids and closed, contiguous ranges")
+        end = row["end"]
+    if not rows or end != s["total_measures"] or s["boundaries"] != [x["start"] for x in rows] + [end + 1]:
+        raise ValueError("segmentation boundaries/total_measures disagree")
+    return s
+
+
+def chinese_number(n):
+    digits = "零一二三四五六七八九"
+    if n < 10:
+        return digits[n]
+    if n < 100:
+        return (digits[n // 10] if n >= 20 else "") + "十" + (digits[n % 10] if n % 10 else "")
+    return str(n)
+
+
+def values(d, s=None):
+    s = s or segmentation(d)
+    n = chinese_number(len(s["segments"]))
+    source = d["segmentation_source"]
+    return {"WORK": name(d), "WORK_WITH_KEY": name(d, True), "COMPOSER": d["composer"],
+            "SEGMENTATION_SOURCE": source + "冻结" + n + "段",
+            "SEGMENTATION_LIST_SOURCE": source + "冻结的" + n + "段", "NSEG_ZH": n}
+
+
+def replace(text, params):
+    # One pass: never reinterpret placeholders appearing in quoted model/source text.
+    return re.sub(r"\{([A-Z_]+)\}", lambda m: str(params.get(m[1], m[0])), text)
+
+
+def render(text, d, s=None):
+    return replace(text, values(d, s))
+
+
+def source_dir(d):
+    return Path(d.get("a_sources") or d["_dir"] / "A")
+
+
+def selected(default=("op60", "op61"), work=None):
+    value = work or os.environ.get("WORK")
+    return value.split(",") if value else list(default)
+
+
+def write_prompts(directory, prompts):
+    """Exclusive output, so offline checks cannot overwrite historical evidence."""
+    out = Path(directory)
+    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    for filename, text in prompts.items():
+        with (out / filename).open("x", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as f:
+            f.write(text)

FILE D:\musicclaude\_thread_evo_20261019\center_dag.py
"""DAG node N04 (10-31, user: "先把这个 center 先试试"; the old N04b fed per-piece history candidates and an example
sentence, banned body/scene words, and Op.61's Center came out as a near copy of the example: "向外行动与旧梦…", specificity 47%).
Three steps, piece-agnostic, no examples, no history:
  write : from the A-draft consensus clusters (N04a, reused: evaluations/centers/A_<w>.json) write the minimal complete
          relation sentence that NAMES ITS DOMAIN (what the matter is about) — keep the concrete objects the drafts name.
          ids cdw_<w> (GPT-6).
  spec  : specificity, the Center sentence alone, all candidates of both works shuffled together; probability on the right
          piece among 10 (centersel.SPEC). ids cds_<judge>_<s>.
  fit   : explanatory power + room to develop per segment (centersel.FIT). ids cdf_<w>_<judge>.
  report: evaluations/center_dag/<w>.md, candidates = new + the current Center (OLD).
usage: python center_dag.py write | spec | fit | report"""
import argparse, json, random, sys
import work_meta
from collections import defaultdict
from statistics import mean
import centers, centersel, evo, seg

OUT = evo.ROOT / "evaluations" / "center_dag"
WORK = {w: work_meta.name(work_meta.load(w)) for w in ("op60", "op61")}
OLD = {w: work_meta.load(w)["old_center"] for w in ("op60", "op61")}

WRITE = """任务：为{WORK}确定 Center。Center 是一条“线”所围绕的那件人的事：什么对这个人重要，又是什么使它难以安顿。它是读者听完全曲回头看时，把各段组织起来的那件事。

写成一句“最小完整关系句”：
- 主语：一个或几个对象，用名词短语直接说出它们是什么。
- 要点出领域：读者单看这句话，就要知道这件事是关于什么的、落在哪种关系或处境里。下面材料里说出的具体对象，尽量保留它们本来的名字，不要换成更空的上位词；不能只剩形式上的词（只说“做事”“想念”“内心”“向外”，却不说是关于什么的）。
- 谓语：说清这几个对象之间的一个关系，这个关系从曲子较早处就成立，一直到结尾都成立。不要为了戏剧性默认冲突；材料里是冲突就写明。
- 不写人称和心理动词（他、我、想、怕……）；不写后来才出现的变化、认识、解决或结局；不用比喻和画面；不写问句；不超过 30 个汉字。

依据：下面是 24 份独立文章对这首曲子中心理解的聚类结果（括号里是支持的份数）。贴着支持最多的那一类写，不要换成新奇角度。如果另一类也有很广的独立支持，而且确实是另一件事，可以另写一个；同一件事换个说法只算一个。
{CLUSTERS}

每个 Center 先给 3 种写法，再选一种，并用一句话说出它点出的领域是什么。
只输出一个 ```json 代码块：{{"centers": [{{"options": ["…", "…", "…"], "center": "选中的一句", "domain": "这句话点出的领域", "basis": "依据哪一类、几份"}}]}}"""


# v2 (10-31, user on "失去后的自尊与行动力始终受回忆和脆弱牵制": 失去啥了 / 为什么是受…牵制而不是平等 / 牵制就是最精确的吗):
# feed the 24 per-draft cores (cluster summaries had already dropped the objects), make the model count objects and the
# drafts' own relation verbs first, keep the predicate true for the whole piece incl. the ending, no default verbs.
WRITE2 = """任务：为{WORK}确定 Center。Center 是一条“线”所围绕的那件人的事：什么对这个人重要，又是什么使它难以安顿。它是读者听完全曲回头看时，把各段组织起来的那件事。

材料：24 篇独立文章，每篇对这首曲子中心理解的一句话概括（文章来自不同的人，彼此没有商量）：
{CORES}

请按下面的步骤做，前三步是给你自己理清材料用的，也要写出来：
1. 对象：列出材料里反复出现的“对象”——这个人在意、失去、守住、渴望或抗拒的东西。每个写出几篇提到，并说明材料对它的叫法是否一致（例如有的说出了具体是什么，有的只说“某种东西”）。
2. 关系动词：列出材料用来描述这些对象之间关系的动词或说法（原词），每个写出几篇用到。
3. 从头到尾：这几个对象之间，有没有一个从曲子较早处一直到结尾都成立的关系？特别看结尾：到结尾时，各方是什么状态（被战胜、被接纳、合在一起、仍在拉扯……）？只算多数材料都支持的。
4. 写 Center：一句“最小完整关系句”，不超过 30 个汉字。
   - 名词：说得出它究竟是什么。材料里叫法一致的，用材料的叫法；不一致的，用支持最多、多数材料都能接受的说法，并在 why 里写明几篇支持；不能留下没有对象的空词（单说“失去”“牵挂”“过去”“某种东西”而不说是什么的）。
   - 谓语：必须对全曲成立，包括结尾；各方的地位按材料来——不要把一方定成另一方的障碍或附属，除非多数材料从头到尾都这样写；动词从第 2 步的材料用词里选，或者贴近它们，并在 why 里说明它为什么比其他候选动词更准确。“牵制”“张力”“并存”“交织”这类放在哪首曲子上都说得通的词，除非你能说明它比材料用词更准确，否则不用。
   - 不写人称和心理动词（他、我、想、怕……）；不写后来才出现的变化、认识或结局；不用比喻和画面；不写问句。
5. 自检：一个没读过这些材料的人，单看这句话，能不能说出这件事是关于什么的？把它放到另一首{COMPOSER}作品上是否也成立？如果也成立，就改得更贴这首曲子。

如果材料里另有一种理解也有很广的独立支持，而且确实是另一件事，可以另写一个；同一件事换个说法只算一个。每个 Center 给 3 种写法，再选一种。
只输出一个 ```json 代码块：
{{"objects": [{{"object": "…", "n": 0, "naming": "叫法是否一致"}}], "verbs": [{{"verb": "…", "n": 0}}], "whole": "从头到尾成立的关系，以及结尾时各方的状态",
  "centers": [{{"options": ["…", "…", "…"], "center": "选中的一句", "domain": "这句话点出的领域", "why": "名词和谓语为什么这样选（几篇支持、为什么这个动词最准确）", "selfcheck": "第 5 步的结果"}}]}}"""


def cores(w):
    A = json.loads((evo.ROOT / "evaluations" / "centers" / f"A_{w}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    return "\n".join(f"{k}. {x['core']}" for k, x in enumerate(A["per_draft"], 1))


def clusters(w):
    A = json.loads((evo.ROOT / "evaluations" / "centers" / f"A_{w}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    return "\n".join(f"- （{c['n']} 份）{c['summary']}" for c in A["clusters"])


V = __import__("os").environ.get("CDV", "")
WID = lambda w: f"cdw{V}_{w}"


def pool(w):
    j = evo.parse_json(evo.output(WID(w)) or "{}") or {}
    return [{"id": f"N{k}", "center": c["center"], "domain": c.get("domain", ""), "src": "新流程"} for k, c in enumerate(j.get("centers") or [], 1)] \
        + ([{"id": "OLD", "center": work_meta.load(w)["old_center"], "domain": "", "src": "现在的 Center"}] if work_meta.load(w).get("old_center") else [])


def render_write(w, version=None, material=None):
    version = V if version is None else version
    T = globals().get(f"WRITE{version}") or WRITE
    if material is None:
        material = json.loads((evo.ROOT / "evaluations" / "centers" / f"A_{w}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    d = work_meta.load(w)
    return T.format(WORK=work_meta.name(d), COMPOSER=d["composer"],
                    CLUSTERS="\n".join(f"- （{c['n']} 份）{c['summary']}" for c in material["clusters"]),
                    CORES="\n".join(f"{k}. {x['core']}" for k, x in enumerate(material["per_draft"], 1)))


def spec_template(works):
    ds = [work_meta.load(w) for w in works]
    if any((d["composer"], d["specificity_candidates"]) != (ds[0]["composer"], ds[0]["specificity_candidates"]) for d in ds):
        raise ValueError("joint specificity requires identical composer and candidate lists; run --work separately")
    return centersel.spec_for(works[0])

    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()

    elif ph == "spec":
        items = [{"id": f"{w}:{c['id']}", "center": c["center"]} for w in works for c in pool(w)]
        jobs = []
        for jn, model in (("gpt6", evo.G6), ("gpt56", evo.G56)):
            for s in (1, 2):

                S = spec_template(works)
                msg = S + "\n".join(f"C{i + 1}：{x['center']}" for i, x in enumerate(it))
                rid = f"cds{V}{scope}_{jn}_{s}"
                jobs.append((rid, (lambda rid=rid, msg=msg, model=model: evo.call(rid, msg, prompt_text=S, model=model, meta={"stage": "center_dag_spec"}))))
        evo.run_parallel(jobs, 4)
    elif ph == "fit":
        jobs = []
        for w in works:
            p = pool(w)

                pp = p[:]; random.Random(f"cdf{w}{jn}").shuffle(pp)

        evo.run_parallel(jobs, 4)
    elif ph == "report":
        spec = defaultdict(list)
        for jn in ("gpt6", "gpt56"):
            for s in (1, 2):
                r = evo.output(f"cds{V}{scope}_{jn}_{s}")

                        w = cid.split(":")[0]; tot = sum(float(v) for v in it["probs"].values()) or 1

        for w in works:
            fit = defaultdict(list)

                        fit[c["id"]].append({"nat": bs.count("自然") / max(1, len(bs)), "no": bs.count("不能"), "dev": float(c.get("development", 0)),
                                             "ov": float(c.get("overall", 0)), "note": c.get("note", "")})
            m = lambda xs: mean(xs) if xs else float("nan")

            for c in pool(w):
                f = fit.get(c["id"], []); sp = spec.get(f"{w}:{c['id']}", [])
                md.append(f"| {c['id']} | {c['src']} | {m(sp):.0f}% | {100 * m([x['nat'] for x in f]):.0f}% | {m([x['no'] for x in f]):.1f} | "
                          f"{m([x['dev'] for x in f]):.1f} | {m([x['ov'] for x in f]):.1f} | {c['center']} | {c['domain']} |")
            md += ["", "评委一句话："] + [f"- {cid}（{'gpt-6' if k == 0 else '5.6'}）：{x['note']}" for cid, xs in fit.items() for k, x in enumerate(xs)]
            (OUT / f"{w}{V}.md").write_text("\n".join(md), encoding="utf-8"); print("\n".join(md)); print()


FILE D:\musicclaude\_bead_op61_20261020\chain.py
"""Op.61 copy of bead_chain/chain.py (only the pool stage is used here; Op.60 original untouched).
Op.60 A Pool -> Bead/STRUCT reusable chain (all model work via local gpt3 / GPT Web).

Stages (each cached per segment under runs/<tag>/<stage>/):
  pool   : gpt3 extracts every A passage applicable to the segment (exhaustive ledger)
  synth  : gpt3 turns pool -> STRUCT + reader bead (every pool item must be dispositioned)
  audit  : independent gpt3 audit; compares blind-labelled versions against its own experience list
  report : deterministic checks + metrics + deliverable assembly (no model, no human edits)

Usage:
  python chain.py pool   --tag v2 --segs S02,S07
  python chain.py synth  --tag v2 --segs S02,S07
  python chain.py audit  --tag v2 --segs S02,S07 --targets short,v1,v2
  python chain.py report --tag v2
  python chain.py all    --tag v2 --segs all --targets short,v1,v2
Prompt versions are chosen in chain_config.json.
"""
import argparse, os, hashlib, json, random, re, subprocess, sys, threading, time, zipfile
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
CFG = json.loads((ROOT / "chain_config.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
GPT3 = CFG["gpt3"]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT.parent / "_thread_evo_20261019"))
import work_meta
META = None
INP = ROOT / "inputs"
SEGS = {}; SHORT = {}; A = {}; NA = {}


def configure(work=None):
    global ROOT, META, INP, SEGS, SHORT, A, NA
    META = work_meta.load(work or os.environ.get("WORK", "op61"))
    sd = work_meta.segmentation(META)
    # Preserve old paths for registered experiments; new works have their own cache namespace.
    ROOT = Path(META.get("bead_legacy_root") or META["_dir"] / "bead")
    INP = ROOT / "inputs"
    SEGS = {x["id"]: (f"{x['start']}-{x['end']}", x.get("label", "")) for x in sd["segments"]}
    SHORT = {x["segment"]: x for x in json.loads((INP / "short_beads_ref.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))} if (INP / "short_beads_ref.json").exists() else {}
    A = {p.stem: p.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig") for p in sorted(work_meta.source_dir(META).glob("*.md"))}
    NA = {k: norm(v) for k, v in A.items()}
    return sd



def mmr(seg):
    return SEGS[seg][0].replace("-", "–")


def norm(s):
    return re.sub(r"[\s\*`“”\"'‘’「」『』《》，。、；：！？,.;:!?（）()\-—–…·]", "", s or "")


def quote_ok(src, q):
    q = norm(q)
    return bool(q) and src in NA and q in NA[src]


def span(s):
    m = re.findall(r"\d+", s or "")
    return (int(m[0]), int(m[-1])) if m else None


def overlaps(seg, measures):
    a, b = span(SEGS[seg][0]); sp = span(measures)
    return sp is None or (sp[0] <= b and sp[1] >= a)


def in_seg(seg, measures):
    a, b = span(SEGS[seg][0]); sp = span(measures)
    return sp is not None and a <= sp[0] <= sp[1] <= b


# ---------------- gpt3 ----------------
_lock = threading.Lock(); _last = [0.0]


def gpt3(prompt_text, attach, outdir: Path, name):
    for attempt in range(1 + CFG.get("retries", 1)):
        r = _gpt3_once(prompt_text, attach, outdir, name)
        if r is not None:
            return r
        time.sleep(30)
    return None


def _gpt3_once(prompt_text, attach, outdir: Path, name):
    outdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    pf = outdir / f"{name}.prompt.txt"
    pf.write_text(prompt_text, encoding="utf-8")
    with _lock:
        w = CFG.get("min_start_gap_s", 7) - (time.time() - _last[0])
        if w > 0:
            time.sleep(w)
        _last[0] = time.time()
    cmd = [GPT3, "ask", "--file", str(pf), "--json"]
    fdef template(name):
    base = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "prompts"
    file = base / f"{name}.param.md"
    if not file.exists():
        file = base / f"{name}.param.txt"
    return work_meta.render(file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), META)


def production_runtime(history_root=None):
    """Reuse the production tasks and pacing/cache rules without changing sat.py."""
    import importlib.util
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("_parameterized_bead_sat", Path(__file__).with_name("sat.py"))
    rt = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(rt)
    rt.ROOT = ROOT
    rt.RUNS = Path(history_root) if history_root else ROOT / "runs"
    rt.INP = INP
    rt.SEGS = SEGS
    rt.A = A; rt.NA = NA
    rt.SEC_PER_MEASURE = META["seconds_per_measure"]
    rt.SEGLIST = "；".join(f"{s} 第{rt.mm(s)}小节 {lab}" for s, (_, lab) in SEGS.items())
    rt.tpl = template
    rt.LOG = rt.RUNS / "calls.jsonl"

    class Pool(dict):
        def __missing__(self, seg):
            self[seg] = json.loads((rt.RUNS / "pool_v2" / "pool" / f"{seg}.parsed.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            return self[seg]

    rt.POOL = Pool()

    def fix_fresh(seg):
        prev = {k: v for k, v in rt.load("final_b", seg).items() if not k.startswith("_")}

        def strip(x):
            if isinstance(x, dict):
                return {k: strip(v) for k, v in x.items() if k not in ("quote_verified", "quote_found_in")}
            if isinstance(x, list):
                return [strip(v) for v in x]
            return x

        head = rt.fill(template("fix_fresh_head").replace("{PREVIOUS}", json.dumps(strip(prev), ensure_ascii=False, indent=0)), seg)
        txt = "```json\n" + json.dumps(rt.piece_for(seg, "piece_b"), ensure_ascii=False, indent=0) + "\n```"
        msg = head + rt.fill(template("fix_v1"), seg, STRUCT_SPEC=rt.fill(template("struct_spec_v1"), seg), PIECE=txt)
        return rt.finish("final2_b", seg, rt.call("final2_b", seg, msg, [rt.sources_zip()]))

    rt.TASKS["final2_b"] = ((fix_fresh if META["fix_session"] == "fresh" else
                            lambda s: rt.t_fix(s, "piece_b", "gen_b", "final2_b")),
                           lambda s: [("final_b", s)])
    rt.parameterized_fix = rt.TASKS["final2_b"][0]
    return rt


def production(a):
    rt = production_runtime(a.history_root)
    segs = list(SEGS) if a.segs == "all" else a.segs.split(",")
    if a.stage == "render":
        if not a.render_dir:
            raise ValueError("render requires --render-dir; --history-root must supply prior stage payloads")
        prompts = {}

        def capture(stage, seg, prompt_text, attach=(), session=None):
            prompts[f"{stage.replace('/', '_')}.{seg}.txt"] = prompt_text
            return None  # finish() makes no writes without a response

        rt.call = capture
        rt.sources_zip = lambda: rt.RUNS / "sources.zip"  # no archive creation during rendering
        for s in segs:
            rt.t_pool(s); rt.t_gen_b(s); rt.t_rev1_b(s); rt.t_rev2_b(s); rt.t_final_b(s)
            prompts[f"struct_spec.{s}.txt"] = rt.fill(template("struct_spec_v1"), s)
        rt.t_piece("final_b", "piece_b")
        for s in segs:
            rt.parameterized_fix(s)
        work_meta.write_prompts(a.render_dir, prompts)
        return
    assert len(A) == 24 and not any(x in k.lower() for k in A for x in ("muse", "opus", "sonnet")), "need the 24 non-Muse, non-Claude A sources"
    rt.run(["pool", "gen_b", "rev1_b", "rev2_b", "final_b"], segs)
    if any(not rt.done("final_b", s) for s in SEGS):
        raise RuntimeError("final_b incomplete: stop before whole-piece review")
    if not rt.done("piece_b", "ALL"):
        if rt.blocked("piece_b", "ALL") or not rt.t_piece("final_b", "piece_b"):
            raise RuntimeError("piece_b blocked or failed")
    flagged = [s for s in SEGS if rt.needs_fix(s, "piece_b")]
    if flagged:
        rt.run(["final2_b"], flagged)
    if any(not rt.done("final2_b", s) for s in flagged):
        raise RuntimeError("flagged fixes incomplete")
    print("N03 complete:", rt.RUNS, "fixed:", flagged)


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("stage", choices=["production", "render", "pool", "synth", "audit", "report", "build", "display", "all"])
    ap.add_argument("--tag", default="pool_v2")
    ap.add_argument("--work", default=os.environ.get("WORK", "op61"))
    ap.add_argument("--render-dir")
    ap.add_argument("--history-root", help="offline render: existing N03 runs directory")
    ap.add_argument("--segs", default="all")
    ap.add_argument("--targets", default="short,v1")
    ap.add_argument("--out", default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--from-tag", default=None, help="display: STRUCT source tag")
    ap.add_argument("--par", type=int, default=CFG.get("parallel", 4))
    a = ap.parse_args()
    configure(a.work)
    if a.stage in ("production", "render"):
        return production(a)
    assert len(A) == 24 and not any(x in k.lower() for k in A for x in ("muse", "opus", "sonnet")), "need the 24 non-Muse, non-Claude A sources"
    segs = list(SEGS) if a.segs == "all" else a.segs.split(",")
    tg = a.targets.split(",")
    if a.stage in ("pool", "all"):
        par_map(lambda s: st_pool(a.tag, s), segs, a.par)
    if a.stage in ("synth", "all"):
        par_map(lambda s: st_synth(a.tag, s), segs, a.par)
    if a.stage in ("audit", "all"):
        par_map(lambda s: st_audit(a.tag, s, tg), segs, a.par)
    if a.stage == "display":
        src = a.from_tag or a.tag
        par_map(lambda s: st_display(a.tag, s, src), segs, a.par)
        rep = {s: display_checks(a.tag, src, s) for s in segs}
        (ROOT / "runs" / a.tag / "display_checks.json").write_text(json.dumps(rep, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
        for k, v in rep.items():
            print(k, v)
        return
    if a.stage in ("report", "all", "audit"):
        st_report(a.tag, segs)
    if a.stage in ("build", "all"):
        st_build(a.tag, segs, a.out or str(ROOT / "runs" / a.tag / "deliverable"))


configure()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
